Result: 在第四届EI-MiGA-IJCAI挑战赛Track 2中取得F1分数0.43808，排名第一。

Conclusion: 时空解耦的双分支设计比单分支联合建模更适用于微手势的细粒度模式识别，自适应增强策略能有效缓解长尾分布问题。

Abstract: Micro-gesture online recognition aims to temporally localize and classify subtle gestures in untrimmed videos. Owing to their extremely short duration, low motion amplitude, and ambiguous visual cues, capturing discriminative spatiotemporal representations remains highly challenging. Existing parameter-efficient adapters typically employ a single branch to model spatial and temporal cues jointly, which may fail to capture the fine-grained patterns of micro-gestures. To address this limitation, we propose a Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Adapter that decomposes video adaptation into independent temporal and spatial branches via lightweight depthwise convolutions. In addition, to address the long-tail distribution problem in the benchmark dataset, we introduce Adaptive Soft Balanced Augmentation, which dynamically allocates augmentation intensity based on class rarity and learning difficulty, without manual thresholds. Our method achieves an F1 score of 0.43808, ranking 1st in Track 2 of the 4th EI-MiGA-IJCAI Challenge.

</details>


### [111] [Watch, Remember, Reason: Human-View Video Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07433)
*Jiahao Meng,Yue Tan,Qi Xu,Kuan Gao,Weisong Liu,Yanwei Li,Jason Li,Lingdong Kong,Haochen Wang,Qianyu Zhou,Jiangning Zhang,Guangliang Cheng,Yunhai Tong,Lu Qi,Minghsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从"人类视角"出发，将基于大语言模型的视频理解系统归纳为"观看、记忆、推理"三大功能能力，系统综述了视频多模态大语言模型在感知表征、记忆状态、推理轨迹和最终预测方面的研究进展、代表性方法、应用领域、数据集与评估基准，并指出了未来开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解研究正从短片段向长视频、多模态、知识密集型场景演进，现有工作缺乏统一的分析视角。本文提出以"人类视角"（观看、记忆、推理）为框架，系统梳理视频MLLM如何获取证据、保留上下文并产生有依据的输出。

Method: 提出一个形式化框架，将视频理解系统刻画为感知表征、记忆状态、推理轨迹和最终预测四个组成部分。据此组织代表性方法：观看涵盖细粒度、全面、音视频和高效感知；记忆涵盖离线和流式记忆；推理涵盖纯文本推理和视频推理。

Result: 系统梳理了视频MLLM在时空感知、长视频高效处理、记忆建模、流式理解和忠实推理等方面的挑战，覆盖了第一人称、体育、教学、医疗和叙事视频等应用领域，以及训练数据集和评估基准。

Conclusion: 指出未来视频智能的发展方向包括可扩展、记忆感知和基于证据的推理，并开放了持续追踪相关工作的仓库。

Abstract: Video understanding is being rapidly transformed by multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as research moves from short clips to long, multimodal, and knowledge-intensive video scenarios. These scenarios require models to handle sparse evidence, long-range dependencies, multimodal alignment, and reliable inference under limited computational budgets. This work presents a human-view perspective on LLM-based video understanding, organized around three functional abilities: watching, remembering, and reasoning. Rather than treating video tasks as isolated benchmarks, this view provides a unified structure for analyzing how video MLLMs acquire evidence, preserve context, and produce grounded outputs. We introduce a formulation that characterizes video understanding systems by their perceptual representations, memory states, reasoning traces, and final predictions. Based on this formulation, we identify challenges in spatio-temporal perception, efficient long-video processing, memory modeling, streaming understanding, and faithful reasoning. Representative methods are organized by their roles in video MLLM systems. Watching covers fine-grained, comprehensive, audio-visual, and efficient perception. Remembering includes offline and streaming memory, while reasoning covers text-only reasoning and thinking with videos. We further examine application domains such as egocentric, sports, instructional, medical, and narrative videos, and cover training datasets and evaluation benchmarks across task types, supervision formats, modalities, and capability dimensions. Finally, we outline open problems and future directions for scalable, memory-aware, and evidence-grounded video intelligence. Related works will be continuously traced at https://github.com/marinero4972/Awesome-HumanView-VideoUnderstanding.

</details>


### [112] [Dash2Sim: Closed-Loop Driving Simulation from in-the-wild Dashcam Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07366)
*Anurag Ghosh,Francesco Pittaluga,Khiem Vuong,Angela Chen,Juan Alvarez-Padilla,Manmohan Chandraker,Srinivasa Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dash2Sim框架可将单目行车记录仪视频转化为精确的4D驾驶日志，并构建了包含4,244个场景、270万个3D物体的ROADWork4D基准数据集。实验发现，现有规划器在施工区场景中表现不佳，难以完成临时车道变更，而Dash2Sim恢复的密集深度可将新视角合成质量提升高达19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行车记录仪视频覆盖了比现有自动驾驶模拟数据集更广泛的地点和场景（包括长尾场景），但由于从单目野外视频中恢复精确的4D场景非常困难，这些视频在模拟中难以被有效利用。施工区正是一类行车记录仪捕获的长尾场景。

Method: 提出Dash2Sim框架，将野外单目行车记录仪视频转化为度量级、地理参考的4D驾驶日志，并与独立维护的地图进行验证，无需标注即可兼容现有模拟器。将该框架应用于大规模视频语料库，构建ROADWork4D基准数据集。

Result: 构建了ROADWork4D数据集，包含4,244个场景、270万个3D物体，覆盖17个城市。在验证子集ROADWork4D-CL（2,201个场景）上，基于规则和混合的规划器优于学习型规划器，但所有规划器在施工区场景中均难以完成临时车道变更。Dash2Sim恢复的密集深度在新视角合成质量上比基线提升高达19%。

Conclusion: 施工区场景对现有规划器具有挑战性，Dash2Sim框架有望为基于单目视频的闭环传感器模拟提供丰富的条件输入，提升自动驾驶仿真能力。

Abstract: Self-driving simulations typically rely on data collected in a small number of cities or on hand-authored synthetic scenarios. Dashcam videos cover a far broader range of locations and situations, including rare or long-tailed scenarios. They are considered less usable for simulation because it is difficult to recover accurate 4D scenes from monocular in-the-wild videos. Work zones are one such class of long-tailed situations that dashcams capture. We present Dash2Sim, a framework that turns in-the-wild monocular dashcam videos into metric, geo-referenced 4D driving logs compatible with existing simulators, and verifies eachone against an independently maintained map without annotations. We apply Dash2Sim to a large video corpus to create the ROADWork4D benchmark dataset, which spans 4,244 scenes with 2.7M 3D objects across 17 cities. On a verified subset ROADWork4D-CL (2,201 scenes), we study privileged closed-loop planners and find that work zone scenarios are difficult: while rule-based and hybrid planners generalize better than learning-based ones, all fall short, failing to make the lane changes that temporary work zone channels require. Beyond planning, dense depth recovered by Dash2Sim improves novel-view synthesis quality by up to 19% on perceptual metrics, suggesting its potential to provide rich conditioning for closed-loop sensor simulation from monocular videos.

</details>


### [113] [TEVI: Text-Conditioned Editing of Visual Representations via Sparse Autoencoders for Improved Vision-Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07451)
*Sweta Mahajan,Sukrut Rao,Jiahao Xie,Alexander Koller,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TEVI框架利用稀疏自编码器解耦图像嵌入，通过caption信号选择性地保留图像中的描述信息，丢弃无关信息，从而改善视觉-语言模型中的图文嵌入对齐问题，在多个检索基准上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）的图像和文本嵌入之间存在对齐不佳的问题，根本原因是图像包含的信息远多于其对应caption所描述的内容，导致信息不平衡，影响下游任务性能。

Method: 提出TEVI框架：使用稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoders）对图像嵌入进行解耦，训练一个掩码模块，根据给定的caption选择性地重构图像嵌入，保留caption描述的属性同时丢弃无关信息。

Result: 在合成caption的受控实验中，TEVI能有效保留caption描述的属性并丢弃其他属性。在自然图像上，TEVI在粗粒度短caption（MS COCO, Flickr）和细粒度长caption（IIW, DOCCI）基准上均提升了检索性能，且在更丰富的caption上提升更显著，同时在RoCOCO基准上展现了更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TEVI通过利用caption作为信号来引导图像嵌入的选择性保留，有效缓解了图文嵌入空间的不对齐问题，验证了以caption为指导的信息筛选策略的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP are highly useful for diverse tasks due to their shared image-text embedding space. Despite this, the image and text embeddings are often poorly aligned, affecting downstream performance. Recent work has shown that this can be attributed to an information imbalance: images contain more information than their captions describe. In this work, we propose TEVI, a framework that uses captions as a signal for what to retain from image embeddings. Specifically, we use sparse autoencoders to disentangle image embeddings and train a masking module to selectively reconstruct the embedding based on a given caption. In a controlled setup with synthetic captions, we show that TEVI is effective at preserving caption-described attributes while discarding others. By applying TEVI to CLIP models trained on natural images, we further achieve improved retrieval performance across coarse-grained short-caption (MS COCO, Flickr) and fine-grained long-caption (IIW, DOCCI) benchmarks, with stronger gains on richer captions, and improved robustness on the RoCOCO benchmark.

</details>


### [114] [Mind the Gap: Disentangling Performance Bottlenecks in Video Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07394)
*Danial Hamdi,Fardin Ayar,Mahdi Javanmardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个诊断框架，通过整数线性规划（ILP）将身份和类分配形式化，从而分层隔离视频实例分割（VIS）中分类、分割和追踪目标的误差来源。对七种VIS方法（涵盖在线和离线范式）在YouTube-VIS 2019/2021及OVIS子集上的分析表明：跟踪不稳定性是在线方法的关键瓶颈（在严重遮挡下AP差距超过20），且随视频长度和实例密度急剧增长；语义分类在标准基准上贡献显著，但在跟踪失败严重时影响可忽略；更强的主干网络提升默认分数但几乎不改变AP跟踪差距，证实时间脆弱性是算法性问题而非表征性问题。同时引入可视化工具TrackLens，将差距幅度映射为可观察的查询级故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIS任务中分类、分割和跟踪目标联合评估，但各自对性能损失的贡献不透明，缺乏系统诊断方法。

Method: 提出基于ILP的诊断框架，将身份和类分配形式化，实现模型无关的层级错误隔离；引入TrackLens可视化工具。

Result: 在线方法在严重遮挡下AP跟踪差距超过20；跟踪差距随视频长度和实例密度急剧增加；更强主干网络几乎不改变AP跟踪差距。

Conclusion: VIS的核心挑战是鲁棒的长期时间关联，且该挑战是算法性而非表征性的，需针对性设计解决方案。

Abstract: In Video Instance Segmentation (VIS), classification, segmentation, and tracking objectives are jointly evaluated, but their individual contributions to performance loss remain opaque. We introduce a diagnostic framework that formulates identity and class assignment as an Integer Linear Program (ILP), yielding a model-agnostic oracle that hierarchically isolates each error source. Applied to seven VIS methods spanning online and offline paradigms across YouTube-VIS 2019/2021 and a diagnostic subset of OVIS, our analysis reveals a consistent picture. Tracking instability is a critical bottleneck for online methods, with gaps exceeding 20 AP under heavy occlusion, and grows sharply with video length and instance density. While semantic classification contributes meaningfully on standard benchmarks, its impact becomes negligible where tracking fails most. Although stronger backbones substantially lift default scores, they leave AP tracking gaps largely intact, confirming that temporal fragility is algorithmic rather than purely representational. To complement the oracle, we introduce TrackLens, a visual tool that translates gap magnitude into observable, query-level failure modes. Together, these tools provide a systematic foundation for targeting VIS's core challenge: robust long-term temporal association.

</details>


### [115] [RealDocBench: A Benchmark for Field-Level QA and Layout Understanding on Real-World Regulated Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07401)
*Ameya Joshi,Joon Kim,Gus Eggert,Joseph Bajor,Cindy Hao,Jing Reyhan,Kushal Byatnal,Eli Badgio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealDocBench是一个面向真实监管文档的双轨基准测试，包含QA和布局两个任务，评估18个系统后揭示了传统单指标基准无法发现的性能差异、困难领域以及成本-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准多基于整洁的学术布局或合成文本，且仅报告OCR或Markdown级别的单一相似度分数，这与下游系统实际需要的——在杂乱真实页面中提取特定字段的正确值——关联性很差。

Method: 构建RealDocBench双轨基准：QA轨包含1,356个字段级问题，跨4个领域581份文档，使用typed gold_dict和per-field/per-question准确率评分；布局轨包含1,500个人工验证页面，采用9类COCO格式标注，使用含邻接感知分割/合并恢复的匈牙利匹配器评分。在统一的提取-评分协议下评估18个系统（商业解析API、通用VLM、开源OCR模型），并报告准确率、每页成本和缓存延迟。

Result: RealDocBench揭示了单数字基准隐藏的广泛性能差异，发现始终困难的医学领域子集，以及不同操作点之间尖锐的成本/延迟权衡。

Conclusion: 发布数据集、解析器适配器和评估工具，以支持可复现的、字段级的文档解析系统比较。

Abstract: Document parsing systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes, regulated workflows such as mortgage underwriting, financial reporting, supply-chain logistics, and clinical records. Yet most public benchmarks evaluate parsers on clean academic layouts or synthetic prose, and report a single OCR or markdown-level similarity score. Such documents and metrics correlate poorly with what downstream agents actually need: the correct value for a specific field on a messy real-world page. We introduce RealDocBench, a two-track benchmark built from real regulated documents. The QA track contains 1,356 field-level questions over 581 documents spanning four domains, where each question is paired with a typed gold_dict of key-to-value answers and parsers are scored on both per-field and strict per-question accuracy. The layout track contains 1,500 human-verified page images annotated with COCO-style bounding boxes under a nine-class public taxonomy, scored with a Hungarian matcher that includes adjacency-aware split/merge recovery. We evaluate eighteen systems, spanning commercial parsing APIs, general-purpose VLMs, and open-source OCR models, under a uniform extraction-and-scoring protocol, and report accuracy alongside per-page cost and cache-busted latency. RealDocBench exposes a wide performance spread that single-number benchmarks hide, a persistently hard medical sub-domain, and sharp cost/latency trade-offs across operating points. We release the datasets, parser adapters, and evaluation harness to support reproducible, field-level comparison of document parsing systems.

</details>


### [116] [MemDreamer: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Long Video Understanding via Hierarchical Graph Memory and Agentic Retrieval Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07512)
*Cong Chen,Guo Gan,Kaixiang Ji,ChaoYang Zhang,Zhen Yang,Guangming Yao,Hao Chen,Jingdong Chen,Yi Yuan,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemDreamer 提出了一种解耦感知与推理的框架，通过层次化图记忆架构和智能体工具增强检索，将长视频理解转化为代理探索过程。在仅使用全上下文2%的推理窗口下，该方法在四个主流基准上达到SOTA，与人类专家的差距缩小至3.7分，准确率提升12.5分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在处理数小时长视频时面临严重的token爆炸和注意力稀释问题，导致性能急剧下降。

Method: 提出MemDreamer框架，采用增量流式视频处理构建三层层次图记忆架构（基础层捕获时空和因果关系），推理时通过观察-推理-行动循环进行智能体工具增强的层次化检索。

Result: 在四个主流长视频基准上取得SOTA结果，与人类专家的差距缩小至3.7分；推理上下文窗口仅占全上下文摄入的2%，准确率绝对提升12.5分。

Conclusion: 统计发现VLM的逻辑推理与长视频理解性能之间存在强正线性相关，表明智能体能力缩放可作为多模态理解的新范式。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language Models struggle with hours-long videos because processing full-length visual sequences induces prohibitive token explosion and attention dilution. To overcome this, we introduce MemDreamer to decouple perception and reasoning, shifting long-video understanding into an agentic exploration process. As a plug-and-play framework, it incrementally streams videos to construct a Hierarchical Graph Memory, a top-down three-tier architecture for semantic abstraction, anchored by a foundational graph capturing spatiotemporal and causal relations. During inference, the reasoning model employs agentic tool-augmented retrieval, navigating hierarchies, searching nodes, and traversing logical edges via an Observation-Reason-Action loop. Experiments show MemDreamer achieves SOTA results across four mainstream benchmarks, narrowing the gap with human experts to only 3.7 points. It constrains the reasoning context window to merely 2% of full-context ingestion while delivering a 12.5 point absolute accuracy gain. Furthermore, statistical analysis uncovers a strong positive linear correlation between an VLM's performance on logic reasoning and long-video understanding benchmarks, establishing agentic capability scaling as a new paradigm for multimodal comprehension.

</details>


### [117] [DisPOSE: Projected Polystochastic Diffusion for Self-Supervised Multi-View 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07419)
*Tony Danjun Wang,Tolga Birdal,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisPOSE是一个自监督框架，通过将多视角人物分配问题近似为多随机张量空间上的生成扩散过程，并利用可微Sinkhorn投影和超图卷积解码器，在标准数据集和手术室高度遮挡场景中实现了最先进的多视角3D人体姿态估计性能，且对相机布局几乎无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法依赖合成3D姿态目录，在真实场景中因分布偏移导致泛化能力差；多视角多人3D姿态估计中的人物分配问题本质上是离散的，难以端到端优化。

Method: 1) 将离散的多视角人物分配问题近似为多随机张量空间上的生成扩散过程；2) 在去噪过程中使用可微Sinkhorn投影引导解向有效可行分配；3) 通过超图卷积解码器从多视角回归完整3D骨架，显式建模关节间的结构关系。

Result: 在标准数据集上超越当前最先进的自监督方法；在手术室高度遮挡场景的新基准上表现强劲；仅用10%伪标签即可保留99%性能，具有高标签效率。

Conclusion: DisPOSE通过解耦分配与根回归组件并保持可微性，实现了几乎不依赖相机布局的鲁棒多视角3D人体姿态估计。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human poses for multiple individuals from different camera views is a fundamental bottleneck for analyzing interacting behaviors. Existing self-supervised approaches leverage synthetic catalogues of 3D poses; however, this leads to poor generalization in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts. We therefore introduce DisPOSE, a self-supervised framework that approximates the inherently discrete multi-view person-assignment problem as a generative diffusion process over the space of polystochastic tensors. By employing differentiable Sinkhorn projections during denoising, our model learns to guide solutions toward valid and feasible assignments based on 2D image priors. The complete 3D skeletons of localized individuals are then regressed using a Hypergraph-Convolutional Decoder that explicitly models relational structures and articulated joints across multiple views. The proposed approach outperforms current state-of-the-art self-supervised methods on standard datasets and demonstrates strong performance on a newly proposed benchmark featuring highly occluded scenes from surgical operating rooms. Our diffusion-based localization demonstrates high label efficiency, retaining 99% of its performance with only 10% of the pseudo-labels. Notably, disentangling the assignment and root regression components while maintaining differentiability makes DisPOSE nearly agnostic to different camera arrangements.

</details>


### [118] [OpenGlass: Open-Source Smart Glasses for On-Device Event-Based Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07431)
*Pietro Bonazzi,Julian Moosmann,Ahmet Celik,Philipp Mayer,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种开源智能眼镜平台，采用模块化FPC转接板设计支持事件相机和帧相机，通过硬件-软件协同电源管理（nRF5340协调器+GAP9 RISC-V SoC）实现11.8小时持续ML推理，并在LynX数据集上以R(2+1)D模型达到83.94%的手势识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜受限于功耗、内存和计算资源，且支持事件视觉和嵌入式ML的开源硬件平台稀缺，需要一种适合快速原型开发的新平台。

Method: 模块化设计采用柔性FPC转接板，无需重新设计PCB即可切换事件相机和帧相机；硬件-软件协同电源管理通过可配置PMIC和nRF5340事件驱动唤醒，使GAP9 SoC在推理间隔保持断电；手势识别采用极性分离事件直方图+R(2+1)D模型。

Result: 200mAh电池下实现11.8小时连续ML推理；LynX数据集上R(2+1)D达到83.94%准确率（宏F1=0.781），端到端延迟33.9ms；时间增强和移除模糊类别提升最大（+8.9pp）。

Conclusion: 该开源平台有效解决了智能眼镜在功耗和灵活性上的关键挑战，验证了事件相机+低功耗SoC在可穿戴设备上的可行性，并已开源全部设计。

Abstract: Smart eyewear enables unobtrusive, context-aware interaction through multimodal sensors and on-device intelligence, but is severely limited by power, memory, and compute constraints in a compact form factor. Open-hardware platforms supporting event-based vision and embedded ML at this scale are rare. This work introduces an open-source smart glasses platform for rapid prototyping of novel sensors and algorithms. Its modular design uses a flexible FPC interposer to support both event-based and frame-based cameras without full PCB redesign. A hardware-software co-designed power management system combines a configurable PMIC with event-driven wake-up via an nRF5340 coordinator, keeping the GAP9 RISC-V SoC powered down between inferences. The prototype achieves up to 11.8 hours of continuous on-device ML from a 200 mAh battery. As a demonstration, an egocentric hand gesture recognition pipeline was evaluated on the LynX dataset using polarity-separated event histograms from a Prophesee GENX320 camera. R(2+1)D achieved the best cross-subject accuracy of 83.94\% (macro F1 = 0.781) under leave-two-subjects-out validation, with 33.9 ms end-to-end latency on the GAP9. Temporal augmentation and removal of ambiguous classes provided the largest gains (+8.9 pp). All hardware designs, firmware, and models are released open source.

</details>


### [119] [The Lipreading Gap: Do VSR Models Perceive Visual Speech Like Human Lipreaders?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07435)
*Rishabh Jain,Naomi Harte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了视觉语音识别（VSR）模型与人类唇读者在MaFI数据集上的表现，发现尽管VSR模型总体准确率更高，但其成功和失败的词与人类不同，且模型主要依赖训练数据的语言线索而非视觉感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VSR模型在超越人类唇读性能后，是否真正获得了类似人类的视觉语音感知能力。

Method: 在MaFI词级唇读数据集上，使用词、字符、音素和视位素级指标，将三个VSR系统与人类基线进行对比分析，包括文本n-gram基线、误差分析、混淆矩阵和人机相关性分析。

Result: VSR模型总体准确率更高，但错误模式与人类不同；训练词频比视觉信息量更能解释词级错误；模型在人类认为最难的视位上提升最大，对视觉清晰度的依赖性远弱于人类。

Conclusion: VSR系统主要依赖训练数据中的语言线索而非视觉感知，未能将视觉特征绑定为有意义的词汇，与人类的视觉语音感知机制存在本质差异。

Abstract: Visual speech recognition (VSR) models now surpass human lipreaders on benchmarks, but do such gains establish human-like visual speech perception? To explore this, we compare three VSR systems with human baselines on the MaFI word-level lipreading dataset using word, character, phoneme, and viseme-level metrics. Although models achieve higher overall accuracy, they succeed and fail on different words than humans. A text-only n-gram baseline given only a few initial phonemes rivals human lipreading. VSR word-level errors are consistently better explained by training word frequency than by the visual informativeness of words. Viseme accuracies, confusion matrices and human-model correlations further show that models gain most on visemes humans find hardest, and show much weaker dependence on visual clarity. Our work demonstrates that VSR systems rely primarily on language cues from training data rather than visual perception, failing to bind visual features into meaningful words.

</details>


### [120] [Skill-3D: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic 3D Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07436)
*Haoyuan Li,Zhengdong Hu,Jun Wang,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-3D框架，通过自演进的场景感知技能学习，使MLLM智能体在3D空间推理中根据具体场景自适应选择工具策略，大幅提升工具利用率（从39%到78%），并显著改善3D推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D空间推理的MLLM智能体在不同场景下使用统一的工具策略，导致工具误用和偏好偏差，无法充分挖掘智能体范式的潜力。

Method: Skill-3D框架包含场景记忆和技能库：将成功工具使用轨迹按场景归类存储，聚合蒸馏为可复用的场景感知技能，失败的轨迹作为经验教训附加；当相似场景再次出现时注入对应技能指导智能体，新轨迹的成功/失败进一步优化技能，形成记忆与技能库共同演进的循环。

Result: 在VSI-Bench上工具利用率从39%提升至78%，Gemini-3-Flash在MMSI-Bench上提升67%，Qwen3-VL-8B经技能引导的后训练后在VSI-Bench上提升43%。

Conclusion: 场景感知的技能学习能有效解决3D空间推理中工具使用 heterogeneity 问题，通过自演进机制持续优化智能体的工具选择策略，显著超越非智能体策略。

Abstract: This paper explores agentic 3D spatial understanding, i.e., MLLM agents performing 3D reasoning through tool use. Existing methods often misuse tools and exhibit biased tool preferences under 3D scenarios, leaving the agentic paradigm with only marginal gains over non-agentic strategies. We reveal that 3D spatial reasoning tasks are heterogeneous across scenes, while these agents apply a uniform tool-use strategy to all scenes rather than selecting tools according to the specific scene and task. To address this, we propose Skill-3D, a framework that learns self-evolving scene-aware skills. Specifically, Skill-3D identifies the task scene and records the agent's tool-use trajectory into a Scene Memory, where successful trajectories from similar scenes are aggregated and distilled into a reusable scene-aware skill, with failed ones attached to the skill as lessons. During training, once a similar scene recurs, the corresponding skill is injected to guide the agent, producing new trajectories whose successes and failures further refine the skill, forming a loop in which the memory and the skill library co-evolve. Experiments show that Skill-3D substantially improves tool utilization in 3D spatial reasoning (from 39% to 78% on VSI-Bench), driving the agent toward correct and sufficient tool use. For instance, it improves Gemini-3-Flash by 67% on MMSI-Bench. Furthermore, we conduct agentic post-training over skill-guided trajectories, which boosts Qwen3-VL-8B by 43% on VSI-Bench.

</details>


### [121] [Implicit Data Synthesis for Contrastive Unsupervised Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07498)
*Patrick Kage,Trevor Hedges,N. Siddharth,Pavlos Andreadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过在网络权重空间而非数据空间生成对比样本的方法，用于科学观测数据的无监督表征学习，并在雷达流星观测数据上验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学观测数据标注成本高，现有对比学习依赖数据增强，但科学数据的数据空间扰动会从根本上改变数据结构，需要一种能保持数据原始结构的对比样本生成方法。

Method: 提出在网络权重空间进行扰动来生成对比样本，而非传统的数据空间增强方法；基于SimCLR框架，在雷达流星观测数据上实现并验证。

Result: 在匹配协议下，该方法相比传统数据增强的对比学习方法取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 权重空间扰动是一种有效的科学观测数据无监督学习方法，能够在保持数据原始结构的同时生成有意义的对比样本。

Abstract: Scientific observations generate large quantities of unlabeled data which is laborious to hand-label, making unsupervised learning techniques valuable for processing datasets. Among these approaches, contrastive learning provides a convenient mechanism for extracting structural representations from unannotated datasets. For natural imagery, the general approach is to use a variety of data-space augmentation methods in order to generate synthetic samples; however, for scientific observations data-space perturbations can fundamentally alter the underlying data. Our proposed method is to generate contrastive samples by perturbing the network weights rather than the underlying data, thus more closely preserving the structure of the data. We demonstrate this technique using a SimCLR-based pipeline applied over radar observations of meteors, and show performance gains under matched protocols.

</details>


### [122] [Differences in Detection: Explainability Where it Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07503)
*Johannes Theodoridis,Johannes Maucher,Andreas Schilling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DnD方法，用于直接比较两个目标检测模型，通过匹配算法计算模型间识别的交集、差集和互补集，结合错误类型分析，能更直观地揭示共享和各自错误，并可指导可解释性方法聚焦于度量相关的结构化子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mAP和TIDE等标准指标只能独立评估每个模型的表现，缺乏直接比较两个模型差异的能力，难以直观揭示模型间的共同错误和各自特有错误。

Method: 基于同一匹配算法，计算两个模型共同识别的GT标签交集，进而得到各自识别的差集以及两者都未识别的互补集；将这种比较与错误类型结合，形成标准混淆矩阵来分析检测错误差异。

Result: DnD比独立统计指标的对比更直接直观，能清晰揭示共享和个体错误；与错误类型结合后可自然分析检测错误差异；可指导ODAM等可解释性方法聚焦于度量相关的结构化子集样本。

Conclusion: DnD是一种有实用价值的模型比较方法，最佳应用之一是引导可解释性方法聚焦于度量相关的结构化子集，代码已开源。

Abstract: We propose Differences in Detection (DnD), an intuitive method to compare two object detection models. Based on the same matching algorithm, it complements the standard metrics of mean Average Precision ($mAP$) and TIDE error analysis with the ability to compare two models directly. More specifically, we calculate the intersection of ground truth labels that are recognized by both models, followed by the corresponding difference sets and the complement set of ground truth labels that are missed by both models. The resulting comparison is more direct and intuitive than a comparison of independent summary statistics. It reveals individual and shared mistakes and becomes particularly interesting when combined with error types. In this case, the differences in detection errors can be analyzed naturally in a standard confusion matrix. While valuable in itself, we believe that one of the best applications of DnD is to guide explainability methods such as ODAM towards metric-relevant examples, grounded in structured subsets. The code for our method is available here: https://github.com/JohannesTheo/differences-in-detection

</details>


### [123] [Streaming Video Generation with Streaming Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07508)
*Hanhui Wang,Yiming Xie,Haiwen Feng,Zhaoyang Lv,Shenlong Wang,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamForce是一个流式视频生成框架，通过统一的力表示和蒸馏管道实现了对局部和全局时变力的因果响应，在单GPU上达到16.6 FPS，在力遵循和运动真实性方面达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型针对不同力类型需要分别训练模型、假设力是固定的或依赖非因果处理，缺乏统一且因果的力控制框架。

Method: 设计统一力表示作为控制信号，开发蒸馏管道用于力可控视频生成，采用自回归效率与力响应性相结合的架构。

Result: 单GPU上运行速度达16.6 FPS，在力遵循和运动真实性方面达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: StreamForce实现了对时变力的即时、连贯响应，同时保持稳定的光度真实感和动态真实感，为物理可控视频生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce StreamForce, a streaming video generation framework that enables physically grounded control through continuous force inputs. Unlike prior video models that train separate models for different force types, assume fixed forces, or rely on non-causal processing, StreamForce is a causal and unified model that responds instantly and coherently to both local and global, time-varying forces. To achieve this, we design a unified force representation as a control signal and develop a distillation pipeline for force-controllable video generation. Our model combines autoregressive efficiency with force responsiveness, sustaining stable photometric and dynamic realism. StreamForce runs at up to 16.6 FPS on a single GPU, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both force adherence and motion realism. Project website: https://neu-vi.github.io/StreamForce/

</details>


### [124] [UniSHARP: Universal Sharp Monocular View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07514)
*Meixi Song,Dizhe Zhang,Hao Ren,Ruiyang Zhang,Bo Du,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniSHARP，通过将不同相机系统（从透视到全景）的图像对齐到统一的全景潜在空间，扩展SHARP方法实现通用单目渲染。该方法在特征空间和高斯空间中进行隐式对齐，使用基于射线的通用表示和UniK3D启发的编码器。实验表明UniSHARP在多个相机系统上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHARP方法受限于针孔相机模型假设，无法适用于广角鱼眼、全景等非透视相机系统。本文旨在突破这一限制，实现跨多种相机系统的通用单目渲染。

Method: 1) 将不同图像对齐到统一的全景潜在空间；2) 在特征空间和高斯空间中进行隐式对齐；3) 基于射线的通用表示，高斯原语沿射线和径向距离排列；4) 使用UniK3D启发的编码器提取2D语义和3D空间特征，联合解码生成完整高斯云。

Result: 构建了涵盖多种成像系统并按FoV分层的评测基准，实验表明UniSHARP大幅优于现有方法。

Conclusion: UniSHARP有效实现了通用单目渲染，跨越从传统透视到全景的连续相机系统，验证了统一全景潜在空间对齐策略的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on extending SHARP, the popular photorealistic view synthesis method, for universal monocular rendering across a continuum of camera systems, from conventional perspective cameras to wide-field-of-view, fisheye and omnidirectional panoramic settings. To overcome the pinhole-specific assumptions of SHARP, our key idea is to align various images in a unified omnidirectional latent space. Thus, we propose UniSHARP, which performs implicit alignment in both feature and Gaussian spaces. Specifically, Gaussian primitives are arranged along rays and radial distances in a ray-based universal representation, while 2D semantic and 3D spatial features extracted from UniK3D-inspired encoders are jointly decoded to generate the complete Gaussian cloud. To comprehensively evaluate our method, we construct a benchmark covering diverse imaging systems across various scenes. The benchmark is further stratified by field of view (FoV) to enable fine-grained assessment of the universal monocular rendering task. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of UniSHARP, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin. The project page can be found at: https://insta360-research-team.github.io/Unisharp-website/

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Enhancing Malware Detection with Generative AI: Using Variational Autoencoders to Boost Machine Learning Classifiers' Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06501)
*Mohammad Alharbi,Jeremy Straub*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出使用变分自编码器（VAE）生成合成恶意软件样本，以解决数据稀缺问题，并结合随机森林、XGBoost和序列模型等分类器，显著提升了恶意软件检测的准确性、精确率、召回率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件的不断演进给网络安全带来挑战，新型或罕见恶意软件类型的数据稀缺问题严重制约了检测模型的性能。

Method: 使用变分自编码器（VAE）生成高质量、多样化的合成恶意软件数据集，并将其与原始数据结合，分别训练随机森林、XGBoost和序列模型等机器学习分类器。

Result: 使用增强数据集训练的分类器在准确性、精确率、召回率和F1分数上均显著优于仅使用原始数据训练的模型，对各类恶意软件的检测能力均有提升。

Conclusion: VAE生成的数据增强方法能有效提升恶意软件检测系统的鲁棒性和适应性，证明了生成式AI在网络安全领域的实用价值，并为未来构建更具韧性的检测系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: The advancement of malware poses obstacles for cybersecurity, necessitating the development of advanced detection techniques. This paper proposes an approach to enhance malware detection through the use of a generative artificial intelligence model. Specifically, variational autoencoders (VAEs) are used with the random forest, XGBoost and sequential model machine learning classifiers. Generated synthetic malware samples are used to address the critical issue of data scarcity for new or less common malware types. This approach can be used to augment datasets to improve classifier robustness.
  The proposed methodology uses VAEs to create high-quality diverse synthetic datasets that closely mimic real-world malware data. The effectiveness of these augmented datasets is evaluated by comparing the performance of the machine learning classifiers when they are trained with the original data and when they are trained with the synthetic data-augmented datasets. The results demonstrate a notable improvement in the accuracy, precision, recall and F1-scores of the classifiers, when they are trained using the augmented datasets. The enhanced performance for detecting various malware classes shows the potential of this approach to facilitate adaptation to evolving malware threats effectively. This work demonstrates the utility of generative AI for cybersecurity. It also provides a foundation for future research aimed at developing more resilient and adaptive malware detection systems.

</details>


### [126] [Subtle Injection for Ground-truth Inference of LLM Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06502)
*Abraham Itzhak Weinberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIGIL 是一种通过向受保护文本中嵌入不可察觉的哨兵序列（canary sequences）来检测 LLM 是否使用了特定文档进行训练的框架，基于 Neyman-Pearson 假设检验实现形式化的假阳性率控制，在 36,000 次试验中达到 0.892 的整体 AUC，即使经过 100% 改写仍保持 AUC > 0.86。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容所有者需要取证方法来证明其文档是否被用于训练大型语言模型（LLM），因为越来越多的模型在未经授权的情况下使用抓取的网络数据进行训练。

Method: SIGIL 框架定义了五种哨兵策略（词汇罕见、词汇短语、句法、语义、代码模式），并基于 Neyman-Pearson 假设检验框架构建成员推断分数（MIS），实现形式化的假阳性率控制。通过模拟器参数校准，在 36,000 次试验中评估了注入率、模型大小、哨兵策略和混合比例四个因素的效果。

Result: 整体 AUC 为 0.892，从 0.1% 注入率时的 0.831 提高到 10% 注入率时的 0.947。检测率在 33%-78% 之间。代码模式哨兵 AUC 最高（0.903, Cohen's d=1.84），句法结构最低（0.875, d=1.63）。四个因素对 MIS 均有显著独立影响（p<0.001）。100% 改写条件下 AUC 为 0.864。

Conclusion: SIGIL 框架能够有效且稳健地检测受保护文档是否被用于 LLM 训练，即使经过改写仍能通过语义泄漏保持高检测性能，为内容所有者提供了可靠的取证工具。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained on scraped web corpora without authorisation, content owners require forensic methods to prove that their documents were included in a model's training set. We propose \textbf{SIGIL} (\textbf{S}ubtle \textbf{I}njection for \textbf{G}round-truth \textbf{I}nference of \textbf{L}LM training data), a framework that embeds imperceptible \emph{canary sequences} into protected text and code such that any LLM trained on those documents exhibits statistically detectable behavioural signatures when probed with targeted queries.
  SIGIL defines five canary strategies -- lexical-rare, lexical-phrase, syntactic, semantic, and code-pattern -- and a \emph{Membership Inference Score} (MIS) grounded in the Neyman-Pearson hypothesis testing framework with formal false-positive rate (FPR) control. Simulator parameters are calibrated against the empirical membership inference literature, yielding realistic heterogeneous results across $36{,}000$ trials: overall AUC $= 0.892$, rising from $0.831$ at $0.1\%$ injection to $0.947$ at $10\%$. Detection rates range from $33\%$ to $78\%$ across model-size and injection-rate conditions. Code Pattern canaries achieve the highest AUC ($0.903$, Cohen's $d = 1.84$); Syntactic Structure the lowest ($0.875$, $d = 1.63$). All four experimental factors -- injection rate, model size, canary strategy, and mixture ratio -- have significant independent effects on MIS ($p < 0.001$). SIGIL maintains AUC $> 0.86$ even under $100\%$ paraphrasing ($\text{AUC} = 0.864$), confirming robustness through semantic leakage that survives surface-level rewriting.

</details>


### [127] [MalTree: Tracing Malware Evolution from Embeddings at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06570)
*Akash Amalan,Georgios Smaragdakis,Tom J. Viering*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MalTree是一个受生物信息学启发的框架，利用系统发育技术（UPGMA和Neighbor-Joining）自动建模恶意软件进化关系，达到87%的时间一致性，揭示了恶意软件家族间差异巨大的进化速度，为从样本分类转向谱系感知的进化建模提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的恶意软件检测是反应性的，基于已知样本训练的机器学习模型在威胁演化时性能下降。逆向工程发现家族间进化关系需要数月到数年时间，缺乏自动化大规模分析恶意软件谱系关系的方法。

Method: 提出MalTree框架，借鉴生物信息学中的系统发育技术（UPGMA和邻接法），利用结构、行为和图像特征自动构建恶意软件进化树。引入基于VirusTotal时间戳的时间验证方法，评估推断树与实际进化时序的一致性。

Result: MalTree达到87%的时间一致性，表明推断的进化关系与真实出现时间线高度吻合。部分恶意软件家族的变异速度比其他家族快10倍以上。通过Mirai僵尸网络等案例研究验证了推断关系与已知威胁情报一致。

Conclusion: MalTree为恶意软件分析从逐样本分类向谱系感知的进化建模转变提供了基础，提示应根据家族特定的进化节奏定制检测策略。

Abstract: Malware detection remains largely reactive: machine learning models trained on known samples degrade as threats evolve. Understanding evolutionary relationships among malware families can inform proactive defense, but traditional reverse engineering can take months to years to uncover such lineage relationships. We propose MalTree, a framework that applies bioinformatics inspired phylogenetic techniques (UPGMA and Neighbor-Joining) at scale to model malware evolution automatically using structural, behavioral, and image-based features. We introduce temporal validation using VirusTotal timestamps to assess whether inferred trees reflect actual evolutionary order. MalTree achieves 87% temporal consistency, indicating that inferred evolutionary relationships closely align with real-world emergence timelines. Our analysis shows that some families mutate over 10 times faster than others, suggesting that detection strategies should be tailored to family-specific evolutionary tempos. Case studies, including the Mirai botnet, confirm that inferred relationships from our phylogenetic tree align with documented threat intelligence. Our framework provides a foundation for shifting malware analysis from sample-by-sample classification toward lineage-aware evolutionary modeling.

</details>


### [128] [AgileOS: A GPU Operating System Layer for Protected CUDA Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06697)
*Zhuoping Yang,Yiyu Shi,Alex Jones,Peipei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgileOS 是一个在 GPU 上实现操作系统层保护的架构，通过在库边界虚拟化 CUDA，为 GPU 服务提供隔离的保护机制，防止应用内核直接访问服务元数据、设备队列、MMIO 区域和库内部状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 GPU 应用越来越频繁地与存储系统、网络设备、供应商库和 GPU 驻留服务交互，但当前的 CUDA 编程模型默认将 CUDA 上下文、设备指针、运行时句柄等直接暴露给非可信的应用内核，导致受保护的 GPU 服务需要自行构建临时的接口和隔离机制，缺乏系统级的保护。

Method: AgileOS 在库边界虚拟化 CUDA：应用链接客户端 CUDA Runtime/Driver 和选定的库 shim，而受信任的运行时 worker 拥有真实 CUDA 上下文并仲裁支持的操作。通过指针验证和 PTX 注入的内存访问守卫，将用户分配与受保护模块/MMIO 区域分离。采用模块化设计，支持 cuFFT、PyTorch 等现有库。

Result: 实现了客户端拦截器、worker 端 CUDA 处理器、虚拟化 CUDA 对象表、受保护的 AgileOS 模块、GPU 内存管理器（分离用户分配与受保护模块/MMIO 区域）、选定的受信任库适配器以及 PTX 级内核内存守卫。

Conclusion: AgileOS 提供了一个初始设计和原型，表明通过在库边界虚拟化 CUDA 并引入 GPU 内存管理模型，可以为 GPU 服务提供系统级的保护，同时保持模块化和灵活性，支持现有库生态。

Abstract: Modern GPU applications increasingly interact with storage systems, network devices, vendor libraries, and GPU-resident services rather than executing only isolated compute kernels. This shift creates a need for operating-system-like protection around GPU services, where service metadata, device queues, memory-mapped I/O regions, and library-internal state should not be directly exposed to untrusted application kernels. However, today's CUDA programming model, by default, still gives each application direct ownership of its CUDA context, device pointers, runtime handles, module loading path, and kernel launches, leaving protected GPU services to build their own ad hoc interfaces and isolation mechanisms.
  This paper presents the initial design and prototype scope of AgileOS, a GPU operating-system layer for protected CUDA services. AgileOS virtualizes CUDA at the library boundary: applications link against client-side CUDA Runtime, Driver, and selected library shims, while a trusted runtime worker owns the real CUDA context and mediates supported operations. To protect service state and module interfaces, AgileOS also defines a GPU memory-management model that separates user allocations from protected module/MMIO ranges, using pointer validation and memory access guards via PTX injection. AgileOS is modularized and flexible, supporting a range of protected services and existing libraries such as cuFFT and PyTorch. The prototype includes client-side interceptors, worker-side CUDA handlers, virtualized CUDA object tables, protected AgileOS modules, a GPU memory manager that separates user allocations from protected module/MMIO ranges, selected trusted library adapters, and the PTX-level kernel memory guard.

</details>


### [129] [The Custody Envelope Threshold: Authority-Scaled Admission of External Artifacts in Institutional Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06767)
*Amadeus Brandes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出监护信封阈值（Custody Envelope Threshold）框架，用于评估外部制品的准入安全性。该框架通过四个条件（对象身份、入口路径、撤销能力与执行权限的闭合程度）量化判断直接准入是否合理，并应用于包依赖、CI/CD动作、容器镜像、Terraform提供者与模块、开发者扩展、开放模型制品及MCP服务器等多种现代基础设施制品场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础设施高度依赖外部维护的制品（如包依赖、CI/CD动作、容器镜像等），这些制品易于获取但难以由机构进行准入、治理和撤销，缺乏一个可量化的安全准入模型来评估直接准入的合理性。

Method: 提出Custody Envelope Threshold框架，即权限缩放的制品准入模型。核心逻辑：直接准入仅在对象身份、入口路径和撤销能力相对于授予制品的执行权限充分闭合时才可辩护。当不满足阈值时，机构需采用代理、策略调解、供应商调解、内部化、隔离或拒绝等替代策略。该框架被操作化为四条件有序工具（four-condition ordinal instrument），并与引用监视器推理、最小权限原则和交易成本经济学建立联系。

Result: 框架应用于包依赖、GitHub Actions、容器镜像、Terraform提供者与模块、开发者扩展、开放模型制品六类场景，并将MCP服务器作为留出验证证据。论文还指定了验证设计、确定性预测函数和OSF复制包，用于检验高审查机构是否倾向于对高权限制品实施更强的监护闭合。

Conclusion: 该框架为机构评估外部制品准入提供了系统化的决策依据，有助于在安全性与可用性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Modern infrastructure depends on externally maintained artifacts such as package-registry dependencies, CI/CD actions, container images, Terraform providers and modules, developer extensions, model artifacts, and AI tool servers. These artifacts are easy to fetch but difficult for institutions to admit, govern, and revoke. This paper proposes the Custody Envelope Threshold, an authority-scaled model of artifact admission. It argues that direct institutional admission is defensible only when object identity, ingress path, and revocation capacity are sufficiently closed relative to the execution authority delegated to the artifact. When this threshold is not met, institutions tend to proxy, policy-mediate, vendor-mediate, internalize, quarantine, or reject the artifact. The framework is operationalized as a four-condition ordinal instrument and connected to reference-monitor reasoning, least privilege, and transaction cost economics. It is applied to package dependencies, GitHub Actions, container images, Terraform providers and modules, developer extensions, and open model artifacts, with Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers treated as held-out evidence. The paper also specifies a validation design, deterministic prediction function, and OSF replication package for testing whether high-scrutiny institutions converge toward stronger custody closure for high-authority artifacts.

</details>


### [130] [What Your Posts Reveal: A Benchmark and Agentic Framework for User-Level Privacy Leakage on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06784)
*Zifan Peng,Yini Huang,Aiwen Lu,Qiming Ye,Peixian Zhang,Jingyi Zheng,Yule Liu,Xuechao Wang,Xinlei He,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SopriBench基准（50个用户、1,569张图像）和隐私暴露分数（PES）来系统评估多模态跨帖子隐私泄露，并设计基于溯因推理的Argus框架，在跨帖子泄露场景下PES达0.55，比最强基线提升25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏统一的用户级多模态隐私泄露基准和能够衡量暴露严重程度（超越二元准确率）的评估指标。

Method: 构建SopriBench合成基准（来自Rednote和Instagram的隐私泄露模式），提出PES指标（按上下文敏感性加权粒度），设计Argus训练无关的溯因推理框架（假设形成、证据验证、跨帖子线索聚合）。

Result: Argus在PES上达到0.55，比最强基线提升25%，跨帖子泄露场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: 所提出的基准、指标和框架有效解决了用户级多模态隐私泄露的评估与推理问题，尤其擅长捕获跨帖子累积泄露。

Abstract: Public social media posts can reveal private information through weak cues scattered across text, images, or metadata. Such leakage is often cumulative and cross-post: cues that appear harmless in isolation may jointly expose a user's home, workplace, or routine. However, current research lacks a unified benchmark for user-level multimodal privacy leakage and an evaluation metric that captures exposure severity beyond binary accuracy.
  To address these gaps, we propose SopriBench, a synthetic benchmark guided by leakage patterns abstracted from a private reference corpus of Rednote and Instagram accounts, covering 50 user profiles and 1,569 images with attributes, contextual sensitivity, granularity, leakage type, inference difficulty, and supporting evidence. We further introduce the Privacy Exposure Score (PES), which weights value granularity by contextual sensitivity. Inspired by abductive reasoning, we introduce Argus, a training-free agentic framework for cumulative leakage inference. Argus forms hypotheses from accumulated evidence, verifies supporting evidence, and aggregates cross-post cues into privacy profiles, achieving 0.55 PES, a 25% improvement over the strongest baseline, with the largest gain on cross-post leakage.

</details>


### [131] [AMD-FCG: An Enhanced Function Call Graph Dataset with Integrated Topological Features for Malware Detection and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06815)
*Parthajit Borah,Sakshi Singh,D. K. Bhattacharyya,J. K. Kalita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AMD-FCG，一个增强的函数调用图数据集，通过整合恶意软件的拓扑特征来提升恶意软件检测的效率和准确性，无需动态分析和大量处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件结构复杂，现有检测方法需要大规模数据集和动态分析，流程繁琐，亟需一种可靠且自适应的解决方案来简化检测流程。

Method: 构建AMD-FCG增强型函数调用图数据集，整合恶意软件的拓扑特征，覆盖多种恶意软件家族和良性应用样本，无需动态分析即可用于检测系统开发。

Result: 该框架能够有效简化检测流程，消除对动态分析和大量处理的需求，可用于开发更高效、创新的恶意软件检测系统。

Conclusion: AMD-FCG为网络安全专业人员提供了一种更高效的恶意软件检测途径，有助于提升检测系统的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: As malware illustrates a complex structure and behavior, detection of these has been a significant challenge in the domain of cybersecurity along with related services in daily life. So, it becomes crucial to have a reliable and adaptive solution to address the issue. Among the several detection methods developed over the years, one of the most reliable ones is studying and analyzing the structural and behavioral patterns of malware. These patterns of sophisticated malware can be obtained with the help of Function Call Graphs (FCGs). However, to effectively cover numerous groups of families of malware, it is required to have a sufficiently large dataset for the system to operate on. In order to ensure accuracy and robustness of the system, the dataset should comprise samples of different malwares and a benign application for secure execution of the detection process. This paper introduces AMD-FCG, an enhanced Function Call Graph dataset integrated with topological features of malwares. The framework enhances the detection procedure, streamlining the workflow for cybersecurity professionals and also eliminating the need for dynamic analysis and extensive processing. Therefore, it can be used to develop and deploy more efficient and innovative malware detection systems.

</details>


### [132] [On the Incentive Compatibility of Block Propagation in Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06860)
*Fumichika Maeda,Akira Sakurai,Taishi Nakai,Kazuyuki Shudo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了比特币矿工传播区块的激励机制，发现矿工没有通过传播其他矿工产生的区块来获取挖矿奖励的激励；在第一眼规则下，非多数矿工有动机更快接收和传播区块；不同分叉解决规则在传播激励与挖矿公平性之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币作为无许可系统，需要确保矿工的行为与系统整体利益一致。传播区块是比特币最基础的过程之一，如果矿工有动机不传播某些区块，则表明比特币激励设计存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 基于区块链网络模型，推导了不同分叉解决规则下矿工奖励的解析表达式，显式刻画了区块传播延迟、算力分布和分叉解决规则如何共同决定挖矿奖励，并以此分析矿工改善区块传播的激励。

Result: 矿工没有挖矿奖励激励去传播其他矿工产生的区块；在第一眼规则下，每个非多数矿工都有激励更快接收其他矿工的区块并更快传播自己的区块；不同规则在传播激励与挖矿公平性之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 第一眼规则提供了最强的减少传播延迟的激励，但也最大程度地恶化了挖矿公平性。

Abstract: Bitcoin is permissionless and does not rely on any central administrator, which gives it strong censorship resistance. At the same time, it is important to incentivize miners to behave in ways that align with the interests of the system as a whole. This paper asks whether miners are individually incentivized to propagate blocks, one of the most fundamental processes in Bitcoin. Miners collectively maintain the blockchain by generating blocks and disseminating them across the network. If miners have an incentive not to propagate some blocks, this would indicate a fundamental flaw in Bitcoin's incentive design. Although prior work has studied how propagation delays affect forks and mining rewards, it has not fully characterized miners' incentives to improve block propagation under different tie-breaking rules. To address this gap, we derive analytical reward expressions for each tie-breaking rule based on a blockchain network model that captures the effect of forks on mining fairness. These expressions explicitly characterize how block propagation delays, hashrate distribution, and tie-breaking rules jointly determine mining rewards. We then use them to analyze miners' incentives to improve block propagation. Our results show, for example, that miners have no mining-reward incentive to relay blocks generated by other miners. By contrast, under the first-seen rule, every non-majority miner is incentivized to receive other miners' blocks more quickly and to propagate its own blocks more quickly. Finally, we compare tie-breaking rules and identify a trade-off between propagation incentives and mining fairness. In particular, the first-seen rule provides the strongest incentives to reduce propagation delays, but it also worsens mining fairness the most.

</details>


### [133] [FDM: A Framework for Decision-making to build ML-based Malware detection systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06894)
*Tadiwa Vhito,Jakapan Suaboot,Warodom Werapun,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于构建基于机器学习的恶意软件检测系统的决策框架（FDM），通过加权配置兼容性评分（WCCS）在五个操作参数（平台约束、资源预算、响应延迟、更新频率、检测灵敏度）下对九个配置维度进行排序推荐。实验验证了最优ML配置依赖于具体部署环境的核心假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为恶意软件检测选择机器学习配置是一个复杂的多准则问题，模型选择、特征工程和更新机制需要联合满足不同部署场景下的操作约束，目前缺乏系统化的决策框架。

Method: 提出FDM框架，使用WCCS多准则评分函数，将五个操作参数映射到九个配置维度的排序推荐。在三个数据集（私有Windows API、Malimg图像基准、Android静态API）上进行四项实验验证。

Result: (i) XGBoost在二分类中达到97.46%准确率、<70MB内存，优于LSTM/BiLSTM（最高2.8GB）；(ii) 多分类中经典模型（XGBoost 79.03%）优于深度循环模型（BiLSTM 72.27%），与二分类排名相反；(iii) 类增量学习在11步中仅下降0.65pp；(iv) 迁移学习平均加速2.14倍；(v) 自编码器预处理加速14倍，准确率仅降0.86pp。

Conclusion: 最优ML配置依赖于具体部署环境，验证了FDM的核心前提，对网络安全从业者具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Selecting appropriate machine learning (ML) configurations for malware detection is a complex, multi-criteria problem. Model choice, feature engineering, and update mechanisms must jointly satisfy operational constraints that vary across deployment contexts. This paper proposes the Framework for Decision-making (FDM) to build ML-based malware detection systems. The FDM formalises this selection process using the Weighted Configuration Compatibility Score (WCCS), a multi-criteria scoring function mapping five operational parameters (platform constraint, resource budget, response latency, update frequency, and detection sensitivity) to ranked recommendations across nine configuration dimensions. To validate the framework, four experiments were conducted on three datasets (a private Windows API dataset, the public Malimg image benchmark, and an Android static API dataset). Key results include: (i) XGBoost achieved the best accuracy-to-resource ratio in binary classification (97.46 % test accuracy, <70 MB RAM), outperforming LSTM/BiLSTM which consumed up to 2.8 GB; (ii) in multi-class classification, classical models (XGBoost 79.03 %) outperformed recurrent deep models (BiLSTM 72.27 %), reversing the binary ranking; (iii) class-incremental learning with EfficientNetB0 maintained 99.13 % accuracy with only 0.65 pp degradation across 11 incremental steps; (iv) transfer learning reduced training time by 2.14 times on average for image-based malware data without significant accuracy cost; and (v) autoencoder pre-processing yielded a 14 times training speedup at a cost of only 0.86 pp accuracy. These findings confirm that the optimal ML configuration is context-dependent, validating the FDM's core premise and demonstrating its practical utility for cybersecurity practitioners.

</details>


### [134] [Blockchain Infrastructure for Intelligent Cyber--Physical--Social Systems:Post-Quantum Security, Interoperability, and Trustworthy Data Economies in the Era of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06895)
*Song Guo,Huawei Huang,Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本教程探讨了区块链作为协调层，如何桥接具身AI（基于世界模型的机器人技术）与量子计算威胁的双重转型，提出将区块链从金融基础设施升级为抗量子、可扩展、数据可信的网络物理社会系统基础设施，并包含AWS Braket硬件演示及五个集成模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI的部署对区块链基础设施提出了数据溯源、跨组织治理和激励兼容共享的迫切需求；同时量子计算进展威胁现有密码学原语，需构建抗量子密码敏捷架构以保障长期可验证性。

Method: 通过AWS Braket沉浸式演示（超导、离子阱、中性原子量子硬件）评估密码威胁时间线；设计五个集成模块：具身AI与世界模型需求、量子硬件现实与安全迁移、BrokerChain跨分片架构、基于Croissant元数据标准和机器人学习溯源的可信数据经济、多模态云部署的行业生态集成。

Result: 提供了从ECDSA到后量子签名的迁移演示、跨分片BrokerChain协议、Croissant元数据标准与机器人学习溯源机制，以及开源框架和路线图。

Conclusion: 区块链可作为统一基础设施支撑下一代去中心化智能环境，实现抗量子、可互操作、数据可信的系统架构。

Abstract: The deployment of embodied artificial intelligence via world-model-based robotics presents a transformative opportunity for blockchain infrastructure, establishing urgent demand for trustworthy data provenance, cross-organizational governance, and incentive-compatible sharing across decentralized ecosystems. Simultaneously, quantum computing advances recognized by the 2025 Nobel Prize in Physics and the Turing Award threaten the cryptographic primitives securing these data economies, creating an interdependent imperative: long-lived verification for embodied AI depends on crypto-agile architectures capable of withstanding quantum adversaries. This tutorial examines blockchain as the coordination layer bridging this dual transition, from financial substrate to foundational Cyber-Physical-Social Systems infrastructure that simultaneously secures against quantum cryptanalysis and enables scalable, trustworthy data economies. The session opens with an immersive AWS Braket demonstration engaging participants with superconducting, trapped-ion, and neutral-atom hardware to assess cryptographic threat timelines and witness ECDSA-to-post-quantum signature transitions. Five integrated modules progress from embodied AI and world-model requirements through quantum hardware reality and evidence-based security migration, to scalable cross-shard architectures via BrokerChain protocols, trustworthy data economies implementing Croissant metadata standards and robotic learning provenance, and industry ecosystem integration for multi-modal cloud deployment. By bridging quantum hardware realities with embodied AI data requirements, this tutorial charts blockchain as unified infrastructure for next-generation decentralized intelligent environments, providing open-source frameworks and roadmaps for architecting quantum-resistant, interoperable, and data-trustworthy systems.

</details>


### [135] [DPAgent-in-the-Middle: Agentic Defense and Repair Against AI-Groomed Deceptive Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06914)
*Zewei Shi,Ruoxi Sun,Haoyang Li,Seong Oun Hwang,Feng Liu,Minhui Xue,Xingliang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DPAgent的智能体框架，通过潜在空间净化与防御性提示相结合的方式，主动检测和修复Web界面中的隐私欺骗模式，抵御针对数据空洞利用的新型AI威胁（AI Grooming）。实验表明，DPAgent在检测率（90.98%）、模式覆盖率、页面探索效率及修复成功率（77%）上均达到先进水平，大规模网站研究发现高达98%的网站存在隐私欺骗模式，其中90%以上可被DPAgent缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对Web界面隐私欺骗模式的防御手段存在碎片化、静态化的问题，且越来越易受大语言模型的操纵；同时，Web生态中的数据空洞（data voids）为攻击者注入误导性内容提供了可乘之机，这些内容可被AI系统抓取和学习，从而放大欺骗设计与模型异常行为。亟需一种主动、动态的防御机制来应对这一新兴威胁。

Method: 本文形式化定义了AI Grooming威胁模型，并提出DPAgent框架，该框架编排四个专用智能体，通过潜在空间净化与防御性提示相结合的方式，在实时Web环境中主动探索、检测和修复隐私欺骗用户界面，在用户接触到这些界面之前进行防御性干预。

Result: DPAgent检测90.98%的groomed样本，隐私欺骗模式检测的微F1达到0.816（SOTA）；仅需访问基线方法约10%的页面即可探索超过80%的模式类型；成功修复77%的被检测欺骗界面；对485个网站的野外观测发现，高达98%的网站存在至少一个隐私欺骗模式，其中超过90%可被DPAgent缓解；用户研究证实DPAgent在降低隐私风险的同时保持了良好的浏览体验。

Conclusion: DPAgent展示了面向Web UI供应链的"中间智能体"防御范式在应对欺骗性设计和基于数据空洞利用的新型AI威胁方面的有效性，为保护用户隐私提供了主动、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy deceptive patterns in web interfaces systematically manipulate users into disclosing personal data, yet existing defenses are fragmented, static, and increasingly vulnerable to manipulation by large language models. Moreover, data voids, areas of information scarcity within the web ecosystem, create fertile ground for adversaries to inject misleading content that can be scraped and learned by AI systems, thereby amplifying both deceptive design and model misbehavior. In this paper, we formalize a new threat model, AI grooming, where attackers exploit data voids to seed benign-looking but malicious samples that corrupt model reasoning and normalize deceptive practices. To address this threat in privacy deceptive patterns, we present DPAgent, an agentic and reasoning-aware framework that orchestrates four specialized agents to mitigate the AI Grooming threat via a proactive defense that combines latent space purification with defensive prompting and operates directly in live web environments to proactively explore, detect, and repair privacy deceptive user interfaces before they reach end users. Extensive evaluations show that DPAgent detects 90.98% of groomed samples, achieves state-of-the-art privacy deceptive pattern detection with a micro F1 of 0.816, explores over 80% of pattern types while visiting only about 10% of the pages required by baselines, and successfully repairs 77% of detected deceptive interfaces. A large-scale study of 485 websites in the wild reveals that up to 98% contain at least one privacy deceptive pattern, over 90% of which can be mitigated by DPAgent. User studies further confirm that DPAgent effectively reduces privacy risks while preserving browsing experience. Our results demonstrate the promise of agent-in-the-middle defenses for securing the web UI supply chain against deceptive design and emerging AI threats rooted in data void exploitation.

</details>


### [136] [Zero-Shot Embedding Drift Detection: A Lightweight Defense Against Prompt Injections in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12359)
*Anirudh Sekar,Mrinal Agarwal,Rachel Sharma,Akitsugu Tanaka,Jasmine Zhang,Arjun Damerla,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ZEDD，一种基于嵌入空间语义漂移检测的轻量级零样本框架，用于检测LLM中的直接和间接提示注入攻击，在多种模型架构上实现>93%准确率和<3%假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入攻击已成为LLM应用的日益严重的漏洞，现有对齐方法无法有效防御，亟需鲁棒、通用且高效的检测机制，而非低效的模型特定补丁。

Method: ZEDD框架通过量化良性输入与可疑输入之间嵌入空间的语义漂移（使用余弦相似度）来检测提示注入，无需访问模型内部、攻击先验知识或任务特定重训练，实现零样本部署。同时构建了LLMail-Inject数据集进行综合评估。

Result: 在Llama 3、Qwen 2和Mistral等多个模型架构上，ZEDD在提示注入分类中达到>93%准确率，假阳性率<3%，优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 嵌入漂移是一种鲁棒且可迁移的检测信号，ZEDD可作为轻量级可扩展的防御层集成到现有LLM流水线中，有效弥补LLM安全系统中的关键空白。

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks have become an increasing vulnerability for LLM applications, where adversarial prompts exploit indirect input channels such as emails or user-generated content to circumvent alignment safeguards and induce harmful or unintended outputs. Despite advances in alignment, even state-of-the-art LLMs remain broadly vulnerable to adversarial prompts, underscoring the urgent need for robust, productive, and generalizable detection mechanisms beyond inefficient, model-specific patches. In this work, we propose Zero-Shot Embedding Drift Detection (ZEDD), a lightweight, low-engineering-overhead framework that identifies both direct and indirect prompt injection attempts by quantifying semantic shifts in embedding space between benign and suspect inputs. ZEDD operates without requiring access to model internals, prior knowledge of attack types, or task-specific retraining, enabling efficient zero-shot deployment across diverse LLM architectures. Our method uses adversarial-clean prompt pairs and measures embedding drift via cosine similarity to capture subtle adversarial manipulations inherent to real-world injection attacks. To ensure robust evaluation, we assemble and re-annotate the comprehensive LLMail-Inject dataset spanning five injection categories derived from publicly available sources. Extensive experiments demonstrate that embedding drift is a robust and transferable signal, outperforming traditional methods in detection accuracy and operational efficiency. With greater than 93% accuracy in classifying prompt injections across model architectures like Llama 3, Qwen 2, and Mistral and a false positive rate of <3%, our approach offers a lightweight, scalable defense layer that integrates into existing LLM pipelines, addressing a critical gap in securing LLM-powered systems to withstand adaptive adversarial threats.

</details>


### [137] [HAVE: Host Active Verification Engine for Closing the Contextual Reality Gap in Security Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06968)
*Vincenzo Sammartino,Marco Pasquini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出主机主动验证引擎（HAVE），通过部署安全受限的主机代理，在快照隔离的伯努利试验上进行最大似然估计，测量经验妥协概率 $\hat{p}$，从而弥合安全数字孪生（SDT）中因依赖理论CVSS分数而产生的上下文现实差距。实验表明，HAVE在假阳性场景中降低$P_{\text{reach}}$ 38.2%，在假阴性场景中提升132.4%，净修正+124.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全数字孪生（SDT）依赖理论CVSS分数分配横向移动概率，导致上下文现实差距（Contextual Reality Gap）：未确认的缓解措施使风险被高估，逻辑缺陷绕过内存安全防御使风险被严重低估。

Method: 设计主机主动验证引擎（HAVE），作为SDT扩展，部署安全受限主机代理，通过快照隔离的伯努利试验进行最大似然估计测量经验妥协概率 $\hat{p}$；利用Wilson区间宽度置信权重 $\alpha_w$，通过贝叶斯混合规则（形式上与Beta-Binomial后验相关）将 $\hat{p}$ 传播到蒙特卡洛模拟中。

Result: 在四个漏洞类别、三个安全层级和两个生产二进制文件上的评估显示：HAVE在假阳性场景中降低$P_{\text{reach}}$ 38.2%，在假阴性场景中提升132.4%，净修正+124.1%；校准指数$\kappa$下后HAVE估计变化仅$1.12\times$，而CVSS基线变化达$4.6\times$。

Conclusion: HAVE能有效弥合SDT中的上下文现实差距，显著提升风险估计的准确性，且在不同校准参数下具有高度稳定性。

Abstract: Security Digital Twins (SDTs) provide continuously updated virtual replicas of infrastructure for threat simulation, yet they rely on theoretical CVSS scores to assign lateral-movement probabilities -- creating the Contextual Reality Gap: risk is overestimated where unacknowledged mitigations neutralize exploits, and drastically underestimated where logic flaws bypass all memory-safety defenses. We present the Host Active Verification Engine (HAVE), an SDT extension that deploys a safety-constrained host agent to measure the empirical probability of compromise $\hat{p}$ via maximum-likelihood estimation over snapshot-isolated Bernoulli trials. A Wilson interval-width confidence weight $α_w$ propagates $\hat{p}$ into Monte Carlo simulations via a Bayesian blending rule formally related to the Beta-Binomial posterior. Evaluation across four vulnerability classes, three security tiers, and two production binaries shows HAVE reduces $P_{\text{reach}}$ by 38.2% in false-positive scenarios and increases it by 132.4% in false-negative scenarios, with a net +124.1% correction; post-HAVE estimates vary by only $1.12\times$ across calibration exponents $κ$, versus $4.6\times$ for CVSS-only baselines.

</details>


### [138] [The Sound of Malware: A Memory Forensics Approach for Android Malware Analysis via Audio Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07005)
*Silvia Lucia Sanna,Massimo Palozzi,Leonardo Regano,Riccardo Lazzeretti,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于内存取证和音频波形转换的恶意软件检测框架\approachname，通过将静态字节码和早期执行内存快照直接映射为音频波形，利用频谱描述符、CNN和Transformer嵌入进行检测，在CICMalDroid2020和VirusTotal数据集上达到98.0%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Android恶意软件分析面临鲁棒分类和隐蔽攻击检测的挑战，现代威胁采用代码混淆、动态加载、加壳甚至隐写操纵等高级逃避策略，降低了基于签名的系统和依赖显式语义指标（如权限、API调用、控制流结构）的机器学习模型的可靠性。

Method: 提出\approachname框架，将分析视角从语义程序建模转向基于信号的 structural 表示。通过直接二进制到波形的映射，将静态字节码和早期执行内存快照转换为音频波形，保留低级结构模式，无需反汇编或特征工程。随后使用手工设计的频谱描述符、CNN和基于Transformer的嵌入处理生成的信号。

Result: 在CICMalDroid2020数据集和VirusTotal恶意软件上，\approachname达到最高98.0%的准确率，优于静态声化方法和现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 基于内存取证和信号化结构表示的检测方法能有效应对现代Android恶意软件的逃避策略，通过二进制到波形映射避免对语义特征的依赖，在检测精度上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Android malware analysis is currently facing increasing challenges in achieving robust classification and detecting stealth attacks. Modern threats employ advanced evasion strategies such as code obfuscation, dynamic loading, packing, and even steganographic manipulation of traditional static and dynamic features. These techniques reduce the effectiveness of signature-based systems and degrade the reliability of Machine Learning models that depend on explicit semantic indicators such as permissions, API calls, or control-flow structures. In this work, we propose \approachname, a memory forensics malware detection framework that shifts the analysis perspective from semantic program modeling to signal-based structural representation. Both static bytecode and early-execution memory snapshots are transformed into audio waveforms through direct binary-to-waveform mapping, preserving low-level structural patterns without requiring disassembly or feature engineering. The resulting signals are processed using handcrafted spectral descriptors, Convolutional Neural Networks, and transformer-based embeddings. Experiments on CICMalDroid2020 dataset and VirusTotal malware demonstrate that \approachname achieves up to 98.0\% accuracy, outperforming static sonification and competitive state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [139] [Fast Bounded-Independence Functions and Their Duals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07009)
*Martijn Brehm,Yuval Ishai,Nicolas Resch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究由线性大小电路计算的快速函数，在密码学动机下，构造了快速t-wise独立哈希函数（代数度log₂ t）、满足Gilbert-Varshamov界的快速码及其对偶（具可忽略失败概率、通用域和速率、系统编码、快速通用编码器），以及将任意t个线性独立输入映射为均匀统计独立输出的快速线性函数族（t为任意常数，此前仅知t=1）。这些成果首次实现了电路复杂度与参与方数线性相关的安全多方计算协议，以及最优渐近电路复杂度的加密矩阵-向量积协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学应用需要兼具随机函数特性的快速函数（由线性大小电路计算）。已有成果在t-wise独立哈希、快速码及其对偶、线性函数映射性质等方面存在不足，如代数度与电路大小的权衡未优化、快速码构造失败概率不可忽略、仅支持t=1的线性独立输入映射等。

Method: 通过优化代数结构与电路设计，构造了快速t-wise独立哈希函数（代数度log₂ t）；简化并改进了快速码及其对偶的构造，支持通用域和速率、系统编码、可忽略失败概率；进一步构造了将任意t个线性独立输入映射为均匀独立输出的快速线性函数族。

Result: - 快速t-wise独立哈希函数同时优化电路大小和代数度（log₂ t）；- 快速码及其对偶满足Gilbert-Varshamov界，失败概率可忽略，支持通用域和速率、系统编码、快速通用编码器；- 快速线性函数族支持任意常数t的线性独立输入到均匀独立输出映射；- 首次实现电路复杂度与参与方数线性相关的安全多方计算，以及最优渐近电路复杂度的加密矩阵-向量积。

Conclusion: 本文在快速函数（线性大小电路计算）的随机性质方面取得了系统性进展，优化了哈希函数、编码和线性映射的构造，并将其应用于密码学，在安全多方计算和加密矩阵-向量积中实现了最优或接近最优的电路复杂度。

Abstract: We continue the study of {\em fast} functions, computable by linear-size circuits, that share useful properties of random functions. Motivated by cryptographic applications, we generalize and improve on previous results in this area, obtaining the following results:
  - For any constant $t$, we construct a fast $t$-wise independent hash function with algebraic degree $\log_2 t$ (over $\mathbb F_2$), simultaneously optimizing both asymptotic circuit size and degree.
  - We simplify and improve a recent construction (ITCS 2026) of a family of fast codes with fast duals, both meeting the Gilbert-Varshamov bound. Unlike the previous construction, our construction has negligible failure probability, can accommodate general fields and rates, supports a systematic encoding, and admits fast universal encoders.
  - We strengthen the above to support stronger random-like properties, such as optimal combinatorial list-decoding. This is achieved by constructing, for any constant $t$, a family of fast linear functions that map any $t$ linearly independent inputs to uniform and statistically independent outputs. Prior to our work, this was only known for $t=1$.
  We demonstrate the usefulness of the above results to cryptography. This includes the first nontrivial protocols for perfectly secure multiparty computation whose circuit complexity scales linearly with the number of parties, as well as protocols for computing encrypted matrix-vector products with optimal asymptotic circuit complexity.

</details>


### [140] [MalSkillBench: A Runtime-Verified Benchmark of Malicious Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07131)
*Wenbo Guo,Wei Zeng,Chengwei Liu,Xiaojun Jia,Yijia Xu,Lei Tang,Yong Fang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了MalSkillBench——首个经过运行时验证的恶意AI Agent技能基准测试，包含3,944个恶意技能（按108类三维分类法标注）、703个野外样本和4,000个良性技能。研究发现：代码注入验证通过率94.5%但提示注入仅75.8%；最强检测器对代码注入召回率98.4%，但对提示注入和Agent控制攻击失效；供应链扫描器和提示注入防御各自只看到技能的一半，无法联合推理代码与指令关系。检测恶意技能需要对任务意图、代码和指令进行联合推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码Agent（如Claude Code、Gemini CLI）越来越多地通过第三方技能扩展自身，这些技能同时包含自然语言指令、可执行脚本和工具权限，形成了既非纯代码也非纯提示的混合供应链依赖。现有检测工具从未在这类混合空间上经过经验证的地面真相评估，其有效性未知，野外评估存在偏差。

Method: 构建了MalSkillBench基准：(1) 提出三维分类法（108个单元）对恶意技能进行系统分类；(2) 设计闭合的生成-验证-反馈（Generate-Verify-Feedback）流水线，通过Docker沙箱内的系统调用监控和LLM评判器确保3,214个技能的实际恶意行为被验证生效；(3) 补充703个野外真实恶意技能和4,000个匹配的良性技能；(4) 对现有检测器（供应链扫描器、提示注入防御、技能专用检测器）进行全面评估。

Result: 代码注入验证通过率94.5%而提示注入仅75.8%，验证脆弱性与检测困难正相关。野外样本高度集中：86.6%的行为来自单一加密货币窃取活动，81%来自两个账号。最强技能专用检测器对代码注入召回率达98.4%，但对提示注入和Agent控制攻击性能崩溃。仅使用野外数据进行评估会导致排名波动高达66个召回率点。任何单一检测器或组合都无法恢复代码与指令之间的完整关系。

Conclusion: 检测AI Agent的恶意技能不能仅依赖传统代码扫描或提示注入检测，必须对任务意图、代码和指令进行联合推理。现有工具各自只能看到技能的一半，存在系统性盲区。MalSkillBench提供了首个在该混合空间上经过运行时验证的评估框架。

Abstract: AI coding agents such as Claude Code and Gemini CLI increasingly extend themselves with third-party skills: markdown packages bundling natural-language instructions, executable scripts, and tool permissions. Because a skill is at once code and agent-facing instruction, it introduces a supply chain dependency whose risk is neither pure code nor pure prompt. Detection tools have never been measured against verified ground truth spanning this hybrid space, leaving their effectiveness unknown and wild-only evaluations biased.
  We present MalSkillBench, the first runtime-verified benchmark of malicious agent skills: 3,944 malicious skills labeled along a three-dimensional taxonomy of 108 cells. Of these, 3,214 come from a closed-loop Generate-Verify-Feedback pipeline admitting only samples whose malicious behavior fires inside a Docker sandbox under system-call monitoring and an LLM judge; we add 703 in-the-wild and 4,000 matched benign skills. Our measurements are consistent: code injection reaches 94.5% verification yield but prompt injection only 75.8%, the same fragility that later makes it hard to detect; the wild sample is narrow, dominated by one cryptocurrency-theft campaign (86.6% one behavior, 81% from two accounts) with a small but architecturally new tail attacking the agent control plane; the strongest skill-specific detector reaches 98.4% recall on code injection yet collapses on prompt-injection and agent-control attacks, and wild-only scoring swings the ranking by up to 66 recall points; supply-chain scanners and prompt-injection defenses each see only half of a skill, and no combination recovers the code-instruction relationship. Detecting malicious skills therefore requires reasoning jointly over task intent, code, and instructions. We release the dataset, pipeline, baselines, and results.

</details>


### [141] [From Privacy to Workflow Integrity: Communication-Graph Metadata in Autonomous Agent Interoperability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07150)
*Bijaya Dangol*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出智能体间通信协议（如A2A、MCP）虽保护消息内容，但泄露通信图（谁何时联系谁），该图在智能体系统中具有语义性、前瞻性和可执行性，威胁工作流完整性而非仅隐私。作者提出威胁模型、定义传输层和引导层的隐私属性，评估候选传输方案，并通过A2A案例实验证明：仅凭被动元数据即可显著恢复任务类别，而所提属性组合可将恢复率压制回随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体互操作协议（如A2A、MCP）假设基于HTTP(S)的地址传输，虽然保护消息内容（甚至端到端加密），但通信图（谁何时联系谁）完全暴露。在智能体系统中，这一图的泄露比传统隐私问题更严重：端点带有能力标签、工作流结构化和链式执行、交互关联实际动作，因此攻击者可从中推断待处理工作流、正在组装的任务及后续动作，并能以机器速度在流程完成前采取行动，构成对工作流完整性的威胁。

Method: 1）建立智能体通信图的威胁模型，分析元数据泄露的独特性质（语义性、前瞻性、可执行性）；2）定义传输层和引导层的隐私属性；3）评估候选传输方案（SimpleX/SMP、Tor、混合网络）在属性上的表现；4）以A2A协议为案例，展示元数据保护绑定可在协议中表达但暴露出其身份假设；5）基于真实A2A捕获构建生成模型进行实验，测试被动元数据下分类器对任务类别的恢复能力，以及所提属性组合的防御效果；6）进一步测量攻击者基于元数据泄露采取行动的杠杆效应。

Result: 1）仅从被动元数据（无任何载荷内容）即可显著高于随机水平地恢复任务类别，且仅需工作流的开头部分；2）所定义的隐私属性组合应用后，可将分类恢复率急剧拉回随机水平；3）在固定预算下，基于元数据泄露选择工作流进行攻击的对手，可获得接近于全知攻击者相对于元数据盲攻击者的大部分优势，而同一组属性可抑制这一优势。

Conclusion: 智能体通信中的元数据泄露不仅是隐私问题，更是工作流完整性问题。本文提出的传输层和引导层隐私属性可以有效防御基于通信图的推断攻击和前置行动攻击。现有A2A协议虽可表达元数据保护绑定，但其身份假设仍需进一步解决。

Abstract: Agent-interoperability protocols such as A2A and MCP standardize what agents say to one another, but assume address-based transport over HTTP(S). Such transports protect message content, increasingly with end-to-end encryption. What they leave in the clear is the communication graph: which agent contacts which, when, and how often. In agent systems this graph is more consequential than a privacy framing suggests. Endpoints are often capability-labeled, workflows are structured and chained, and interactions are coupled to real actions, so an observer recovers more than past relationships. It can infer the pending workflow, the task being assembled and the action likely to follow. At machine speed, it can act on that inference before the workflow completes. The threat is therefore one of workflow integrity, not privacy alone: predictive leverage over autonomous action. We give a threat model for the agent communication graph; identify what makes agent metadata distinctively revealing (semanticity, prospectivity, actuation); define transport- and bootstrap-layer privacy properties and weigh candidate transports (SimpleX/SMP, Tor, mixnets) against them; and present an A2A case study in which a metadata-protecting binding is expressible but surfaces the protocol's identity assumptions. We test these on a generative model anchored to a real A2A capture. From passive metadata alone, with no payloads, a classifier recovers a task's class well above chance, from only the workflow's opening; applied together, the properties drive that recovery sharply back toward chance. Beyond what an observer can recover, we measure the leverage of acting on the leak: from a workflow's opening and under a fixed budget, an adversary choosing which workflows to act on realizes in this model most of a clairvoyant attacker's advantage over a metadata-blind one, and the same properties suppress it.

</details>


### [142] [Synthetic APTs: the Collapse of TTP-Based Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07158)
*Francesco Balassone,Víctor Mayoral-Vilches,María Sanz-Gómez,Paul Zabalegui-Landa,Stefan Rass,Davide Quarta,Daniel Sanchez-Prieto,Marina Oteiza-Álvarez,Almerindo Graziano,Lauren Min Kim,MinSeok Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨AI驱动的对手仿真是否挑战了基于TTP的CTI归因基础。通过CSI框架配置5个APT组对抗AI防御者，在20次实验中发现了二元模式：企业网络全部被攻陷（攻击者还独立武器化防御者的Velociraptor作为C2信道），军事网络全部被成功防御或陷入僵局。作者认为AI时代使得个体也能像国家行为体APT一样行动，从根本上动摇了基于TTP的归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证AI驱动的对手仿真是否能够挑战传统CTI归因中"每个威胁行为者留下可识别操作指纹"的前提假设。

Method: 使用CSI框架部署配置为5个APT组（APT28、APT29、APT41、APT44、Lazarus）的AI智能体，在CYBER RANGES提供的企业网络和军事基础设施两个网络靶场中，对抗AI驱动的防御智能体；进行了20次实验，使用两种防御模型。

Result: 企业网络10次实验全部被攻陷（每实验2-12台主机），军事网络10次实验全部成功防御或陷入僵局；8次企业实验中攻击者独立将防御者的Velociraptor武器化为C2信道，该汇聚行为未编码在任何威胁情报配置文件中。

Conclusion: AI时代中，只要提供正确的模型、脚手架和智能体配置，个体也能像国家级APT一样行动，这从根本上动摇了基于TTP的归因方法。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence CTI attribution relies on identifying the Tactics, Techniques, and Procedures TTPs that distinguish one threat actor from another. This approach presupposes that each adversary leaves a recognizable operational fingerprint. This work investigates whether AI driven adversary emulation challenges that presupposition. We deploy agents from our Cybersecurity SuperIntelligence CSI framework, configured as five Advanced Persistent Threat APT groups, APT28, APT29, APT41, APT44, and Lazarus Group, against AI driven Defender agents across two cyber ranges provided by CYBER RANGES, equipped with defensive software Wazuh, Velociraptor, Elasticsearch and active AI driven defenders: an enterprise network and a military infrastructure. Across 20 experiments using two defender models, a binary pattern emerges: all 10 Enterprise range experiments resulted in compromise 2 to 12 hosts per experiment, while all 10 Military range experiments were successfully defended or resulted in stalemates, regardless of APT profile or defender model. In 8 of 10 Enterprise experiments, attackers independently weaponized the defender's own Velociraptor endpoint management platform as a command and control channel, a convergent behavior not encoded in any threat intelligence profile. We argue that in the AI era, wherein agents can be deployed provided the right models are available and subject to the right scaffolding and agentic configuration, the entry barrier for operating like a nation state APT collapses: beyond nation states, individuals can now act like commonly identified threat actors, and with it, fundamentally undermine TTP based attribution.

</details>


### [143] [Rethinking IoT Intrusion Detection: Augmenting Routing Metrics with Radio Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07282)
*Yichang Sun,Andreas Johnsson,Sourasekhar Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在基于LSTM的RPL物联网入侵检测系统中，将无线收发（TX/RX）特征与标准RPL路由层特征结合，相比仅用路由层特征，F1分数最高提升约4%，其中对Worst Parent攻击的检测增益最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的RPL物联网IDS仅依赖路由层特征，只能提供网络行为的部分视图，亟需探索是否引入射频层特征可提升检测性能。

Method: 在LSTM-based IDS中，将TX和RX射频特征与标准RPL特征集结合，在三种攻击类型（DIS-Flooding、Local Repair、Worst Parent）和不同网络规模下评估检测性能。

Result: 结合TX/RX特征后，F1分数相比仅用路由层特征提升约4%，其中Worst Parent攻击的检测增益最为明显。

Conclusion: 引入射频层TX/RX特征可有效提升RPL物联网IDS的检测性能，验证了多层级特征融合的必要性。

Abstract: Machine learning-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) for RPL-based IoT networks often rely solely on routing layer features, which provide only a partial view of network behaviour. In this work, we investigate whether incorporating Transmit (TX) and Receive (RX) radio features alongside the standard RPL feature set can improve detection performance in an LSTM-based IDS. We evaluate the proposed approach across three different attack types, namely DIS-Flooding, Local Repair, and Worst Parent under varying network sizes. The results show that incorporating TX and RX improves the IDS's overall detection performance by up to ~4% in F1-score compared with using routing-layer features alone, with the most notable gain observed for the Worst Parent attack.

</details>


### [144] [Authorized and Verifiable Searchable Encryption Based on Public Key Equality Test for Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07319)
*Xiuping Li,Kaiwen Wang,Xiaolin Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型公钥加密等值测试方案AVPKEET及其搜索加密方案AVSE，支持密文文件级授权、公开可验证性和细粒度访问控制，且无需可信第三方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于PKEET的搜索加密方案缺乏密文文件级授权、公开可验证性或搜索加密层级的支持，无法同时满足云存储场景下的功能性与隐私性需求。

Method: 提出AVPKEET方案实现不可转移、不可重放的密文文件授权与公开可验证性；在此基础上构建AVSE搜索加密方案，设计一次性不可转移令牌（绑定用户和随机数）、批量操作及三种细粒度访问控制模式（ALL/PARTIAL/SINGLE）。

Result: 在标准假设下证明了OW-CCA2安全性、令牌不可伪造性和验证可靠性；实验表明AVSE令牌大小仅168字节，在提供密文文件级授权和公开验证的前提下开销可接受。

Conclusion: AVPKEET和AVSE方案在功能完备性和安全性上优于现有方案，适用于云存储部署。

Abstract: Cloud storage revolutionizes data management but raises conflicts between functionality and privacy. Public Key Encryption with Equality Test (PKEET), an advanced cryptographic technique, can enable multi-user searchable encryption (SE) through cross-key ciphertext comparison without shared keys. However, existing PKEET-based SE schemes lack ciphertext-file-level authorization, public verifiability, or SE-level support. This paper first proposes a novel PKEET scheme, AVPKEET (Authorized and Verifiable PKEET). It enables non-transferable and non-replayable authorization of ciphertext files, while supporting public verifiability, all without the need for trusted third parties. Then we propose an AVPKEET-based SE scheme, denoted as AVSE (Authorized and Verifiable SE), featuring one-time non-transferable tokens bound to users and nonces, batch operations, and fine-grained access control (ALL, PARTIAL, SINGLE). We prove OW-CCA2 security, token unforgeability, and verification soundness under standard assumptions. Experiment results demonstrate that AVSE achieves the most compact token size (168 bytes) while uniquely providing both ciphertext-file-level authorization and public verification, with acceptable overhead for cloud storage deployment.

</details>


### [145] [Defending Jailbreak Attacks on Large Language Models via Manifold Trajectory Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07335)
*Hangtao Zhang,Yucheng Zhao,Sishun Liu,Ziqi Zhou,Zeyu Ye,Wei Wan,Minghui Li,Shengshan Hu,Yanjun Zhang,Yi Liu,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Manifold Trajectory Kinetics (MTK)方法，将LLM视为将输入转化为输出的动力学系统，通过追踪prompt在层间的邻域结构演化来检测越狱攻击。MTK在伪恶意提示和自适应攻击下均表现出强鲁棒性，在多个LLM和越狱攻击中取得优异检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱检测方法依赖于固定度量空间（如原始输入、梯度或隐藏特征）中良性提示与越狱提示的线性可分性，但该假设在两类情况下失效：(i) 包含安全关键词但意图良性的伪恶意提示；(ii) 针对检测器进行显式优化的自适应攻击。需探索更鲁棒的检测范式。

Method: 将LLM视为一个动力学系统，提出Manifold Trajectory Kinetics (MTK)方法。MTK不寻找通用的度量空间，而是分析底层数据流形的邻域结构。通过追踪prompt在各层的邻域结构演化轨迹进行检测：良性提示在整个推理过程中始终保持靠近良性邻域，而越狱提示则呈现出从恶意种子附近开始、随后策略性地向良性邻域迁移以逃避拒答的特征性轨迹。

Result: 在4个LLM和10种越狱攻击下，MTK展现出强鲁棒性：伪恶意提示场景下，在良性提示假阳率5%时实现95%越狱检测真阳率，伪恶意提示假阳率仅2%；自适应攻击下维持85%的真阳率。此外在视觉语言模型上也展现出优越性能。

Conclusion: MTK通过从固定度量空间转向分析数据流形邻域结构，有效克服了传统越狱检测方法对伪恶意提示和自适应攻击的脆弱性，为部署时越狱检测提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Jailbreak prompts can bypass alignment guardrails in large language models (LLMs) and elicit unsafe outputs, making reliable deployment-time detection critical. Prior detection approaches largely rely on a fixed metric space, e.g., raw inputs, gradients, or hidden features, in which benign and jailbreak prompts are linearly separable.
