We show this assumption breaks under (i) pseudo-malicious prompts that are benign by intent but contain safety-related keywords, and (ii) adaptive attacks that explicitly optimize against the deployed detector. To overcome this limitation, we shift our focus from identifying a universal metric space to analyzing the more robust neighborhood structure of the underlying data manifold. We present Manifold Trajectory Kinetics (MTK), which treats an LLM as a kinetic system transforming inputs into outputs and detects jailbreaks by tracking how a prompt's neighborhood structure evolves across layers. Benign prompts remain close to benign neighborhoods throughout inference, whereas jailbreak prompts exhibit a characteristic trajectory that begins near malicious seeds and later strategically shifts toward benign neighborhoods to evade refusal.Across four LLMs and ten jailbreak attacks, MTK achieves strong robustness to both failure modes: on pseudo-malicious prompts, it attains a jailbreak true positive rate of 95% at a false positive rate of 5% on benign prompts and 2% on pseudo-malicious prompts, and under adaptive attacks, it maintains a true positive rate of 85%. We further demonstrate the superior performance of MTK for jailbreak detection in vision-language models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rookie143/mtk.

</details>


### [146] [Empirical Evaluation of Large Language Models for Migration of Code Fragments to Post-Quantum Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07341)
*Javier Pallarés de Bonrostro,Ana I. González-Tablas,María Isabel González Vasco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于合成数据集（800对Python密码代码片段）的复现实验框架，评估大语言模型在代码层面辅助传统密码向PQC迁移的能力。实验表明，领域微调至关重要，微调后的GPT-4.1-mini在静态相似度（0.9072）和动态功能正确率（92.5%）上显著优于零样本基线。在开源项目验证中，该方法对局部模块有效，但在复杂跨模块依赖场景下仍有局限。研究认为，微调LLM结合自动验证可成为密码敏捷迁移管线的实用组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PQC迁移框架主要提供组织层面的指导，而实际的代码级重构仍高度依赖人工且容易出错，亟需自动化工具辅助完成从传统密码原语到后量子密码的代码迁移，同时保证功能正确性。

Method: 构建了一个包含800对Python代码片段的合成数据集，覆盖6种密码族及组合多原语场景，每对通过类别特定功能测试验证。采用零样本GPT-4.1以及微调后的GPT-3.5-turbo、GPT-4.1-mini和CodeLlama-7B-Instruct四个模型进行迁移实验，并通过静态相似度和动态功能正确率进行量化评估。

Result: 微调后的GPT-4.1-mini表现最佳，静态相似度均值0.9072，动态功能正确率92.5%，远超零样本基线。六个开源仓库验证显示该方法在局部密码模块上可产生有效迁移，但在大型复杂项目及跨模块依赖场景中效果受限。

Conclusion: 领域微调对可靠密码迁移至关重要，微调LLM可作为密码敏捷迁移管线中的实用组件，但需结合自动化验证和依赖感知的校验机制。

Abstract: The transition to post-quantum cryptography (PQC) requires not only replacing vulnerable cryptographic primitives, but also refactoring the surrounding software logic. While existing PQC migration frameworks provide organizational guidance, practical code-level remediation remains largely manual and error-prone. This paper evaluates whether large language models (LLMs) can be trained to assist in the migration of pre-quantum cryptographic code fragments to post-quantum counterparts while preserving functional correctness. To this end, we introduce a reproducible experimental framework built around a synthetic dataset of 800 paired Python code fragments covering six cryptographic families and combined multi-primitive cases. Each pair is validated through category-specific functional tests, enabling both dataset quality control and objective evaluation of model-generated migrations. Four models are assessed: GPT-4.1 in a zero-shot setting, and fine-tuned versions of GPT-3.5-turbo, GPT-4.1-mini, and CodeLlama-7B-Instruct. The results show that domain-specific fine-tuning is essential for reliable cryptographic migration. The fine-tuned GPT-4.1-mini model achieves the best overall performance, with a mean static similarity of 0.9072 and a dynamic functional correctness rate of 92.5%, substantially outperforming the zero-shot baseline. A complementary validation on six open-source repositories further shows that the approach can produce useful migrations in localized cryptographic modules, while also revealing limitations in larger projects with complex dependencies and cross-module interactions. These findings suggest that fine-tuned LLMs can serve as practical components in future crypto-agile migration pipelines, provided they are coupled with automated verification and dependency-aware validation.

</details>


### [147] [On the Shoulders of Giants: Empowering Automated Smart Contract Auditing via the GiAnt Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07363)
*Xiaoting Zhang,Zhipeng Gao,Yiran Lv,Xing Hu,Feifei Niu,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GiANT是一个自动化框架，通过从真实审计报告中提取漏洞信息来构建智能合约审计数据集，解决了现有数据集的可扩展性瓶颈和粒度不足问题。该框架在388份审计报告上生成了包含7,711个漏洞发现的数据集，信息提取质量得分达4.76/5，并基于该数据集对多个LLM在漏洞检测等任务上建立了性能基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约审计数据集存在两大局限：人工标注导致的可扩展性瓶颈，以及数据粒度和多样性不足，亟需自动化方法来构建高质量、大规模的数据集。

Method: 采用分治策略结合Chain-of-Thought（思维链）技术从Code4rena审计报告中提取结构化漏洞信息，并引入LLM-as-a-judge机制进行严格的质量保证。

Result: 在388份真实审计报告上生成GiAnt Corpus，包含7,711个漏洞发现（覆盖5个严重级别）；人工评估信息提取质量得分为4.76±0.37/5，评估者间一致性κ=0.88；基于该数据集对4个SOTA LLM在漏洞检测、代码摘要、修复建议和Gas优化任务上建立了性能基线。

Conclusion: GiANT有效解决了现有数据集构建的瓶颈，生成了高质量、高粒度的审计数据集，为自动化智能合约审计研究提供了有价值的数据基础。

Abstract: High-quality smart contract auditing datasets are crucial for evaluating security tools and advancing smart contract security research. Two major limitations of existing datasets are the manual-induced scalability bottleneck and the deficiency in data granularity and diversity. To address these limitations, we propose GiANT, an automated framework designed to curate smart contract auditing datasets by distilling vulnerability insights from real-world auditing reports. GiANT employs a divide-and-conquer strategy coupled with the Chain-of-Thought technique to extract structured vulnerability information from Code4rena reports, followed by an LLM-as-a-judge mechanism to perform rigorous quality assurance. To evaluate GiANT's effectiveness, we run it on 388 real-world audit reports and generate the GiAnt Corpus comprising 7,711 vulnerability findings across five severity levels. Manual assessment of the dataset demonstrates exceptional reliability in information extraction, achieving a mean quality score of $4.76\pm0.37$ (out of 5) with inter-rater agreement $κ$ of 0.88. We further validate the practicality of our dataset by benchmarking 4 state-of-the-art LLMs on vulnerability detection, code summarization, mitigation recommendation, and automated gas optimization tasks, to establish performance baselines, thereby providing a valuable data foundation for future research in automated smart contract auditing.

</details>


### [148] [Lost in Migration: Exposing Android Framework Vulnerabilities in Parallel Java-Kotlin Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07420)
*Rui Li,Wenrui Diao,Debin Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Android框架中Java与Kotlin并行实现的安全影响，设计了ParaDroid分析框架，在AOSP Android 14-16中识别出329对并行方法和37个有漏洞的差异点，已被确认3个漏洞和2个bug，获2个CVE编号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android在采用Kotlin过程中出现了Java与Kotlin并行实现的现象，这些实现功能上应一致但实际存在语义差异，可能暴露安全缺陷。此前缺乏对此类并行代码路径安全影响的系统性研究。

Method: 设计并实现ParaDroid分析框架，将代码归一化为字节码级中间表示，重建类到源码的映射，利用大语言模型推理方法语义并识别行为差异，从而在大规模下识别并行方法并比较其行为。

Result: 在AOSP Android 14-16中识别出329对并行方法对，发现37个有漏洞的差异点，已向Android安全团队负责任披露，3个漏洞和2个bug被确认，获得2个CVE编号。

Conclusion: Java-Kotlin并行代码路径为发现现代Android安全缺陷提供了实用的攻击面，验证了该方法在发现真实安全漏洞方面的有效性。

Abstract: Android has adopted Kotlin alongside Java across apps and core system components. During this shift, we observe parallel implementations in the Android Open Source Project (AOSP) where the same component is implemented in both Java and Kotlin. In principle, their functional purposes are identical. In practice, subtle semantic divergences can appear. Such divergences are not vulnerabilities by themselves, but they provide useful clues that may reveal flaws in surrounding enforcement logic. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first systematic study of Java-Kotlin parallel implementations in the Android framework and examines their security implications. We design and build ParaDroid, an analysis framework that identifies parallel methods at scale and compares their behaviors. ParaDroid normalizes code into a bytecode-level intermediate representation, reconstructs class-to-source mappings, and uses large language models to reason about method semantics and identify behavioral divergences. Evaluated on AOSP Android 14-16, ParaDroid identified 329 parallel method pairs and 37 vulnerable divergences. We responsibly disclosed the exploitable issues to the Android Security Team. Three vulnerabilities and two bugs have been confirmed, and two CVE IDs have been assigned. Our results demonstrate that parallel Java-Kotlin code paths provide a practical surface for discovering security flaws in modern Android.

</details>


### [149] [Verifiable and Confidential DNN Inference on Low-End Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07470)
*Mohamed Khalil Kiri,Ivan De Oliveira Nunes,Aurélien Francillon,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VECODI通过引入SHANGRI-LA执行抽象（基于TrustZone-M TEE），在低端边缘设备上实现了轻量级的机密DNN推理与可验证性，具有较小的TCB、内存占用和运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低端边缘设备上部署DNN推理面临两大挑战：保护模型机密性（防止被受损边缘系统窃取）以及实现可验证推理（确保推理结果正确），而现有方法要么依赖TEE导致高开销和与应用绑定的TCB，要么在非可信环境中执行几乎没有安全性。

Method: VECODI的核心是SHANGRI-LA，一种基于TrustZone-M TEE的新执行抽象，创建了介于安全世界和非安全世界之间的第三运行时环境。VECODI利用SHANGRI-LA将非可信推理代码在非安全世界执行，同时仅使用最小化的与应用无关的安全世界支持来保护模型机密性并实现推理结果的可验证性（包括推理代码和模型参数的正确执行）。

Result: 在真实的NUCLEO-L552ZE-Q开发板上实现并开源了原型，实验结果表明VECODI具有小的TCB、小的内存占用和低的运行时开销。

Conclusion: VECODI是低端边缘设备上安全推理的实用选择。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural network (DNN) inference on low-end edge devices raises two key challenges: protecting model confidentiality against a potentially compromised edge system and enabling verifiable inference without incurring prohibitive overhead. Existing approaches either house partial models and inference software within trusted execution environments (TEEs), resulting in high cost and an application-dependent trusted computing base (TCB), or execute in untrusted environments, providing little security.
  In this work, we present VECODI, a framework for verifiable and confidential DNN inference on constrained edge devices. At its core, VECODI introduces SHANGRI-LA, a new execution abstraction on TrustZone-M TEEs that establishes a third runtime environment with privileges strictly between the Secure and Non-Secure Worlds. VECODI leverages SHANGRI-LA to execute untrusted inference code in the Non-Secure World while using minimal application-agnostic Secure-World support to protect model confidentiality and enable verifiability (with respect to proper execution of inference code and model parameters) of inference results. We realize VECODI on a real-world NUCLEO-L552ZE-Q development board and open-source its prototype. Our results show VECODI's small TCB, memory footprint, and runtime overhead, making it a practical option for secure inference in low-end edge devices.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Optimal Rates for Generalization of Gradient Descent Methods with Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06764)
*Junyu Zhou,Puyu Wang,Yunwen Lei,Yiming Ying,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对深度ReLU网络在梯度下降（GD）和随机梯度下降（SGD）训练下的泛化性能，首次建立了极小极大最优的超额风险率，证明了在足够宽度下深度网络可达到与核方法相当的最优泛化速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经正切核（NTK）框架下的泛化理论研究多局限于浅层网络，深度神经网络的泛化理论存在显著空白。本文旨在填补这一理论缺失。

Method: 在NTK机制下，对深度ReLU网络使用GD和SGD训练，假设网络宽度与深度和训练样本量呈多项式规模关系，分别推导其超额风险的极小极大最优收敛速率。

Result: 首次建立了深度ReLU网络在GD和SGD下的极小极大最优超额风险率；证明了当网络宽度足够大时，梯度下降方法能够达到与核方法同等的最优泛化速率。

Conclusion: 深度ReLU网络在足够宽度条件下，通过梯度下降训练可获得最优统计泛化性能，填补了深度神经网络泛化理论的重要空白。

Abstract: Recent progress has been made in understanding the statistical generalization performance of gradient descent methods for overparameterized neural networks within the neural tangent kernel (NTK) regime. However, most of the existing work on regression problems is limited to shallow network architectures, leaving a notable gap in the theory of deep neural networks. This paper addresses this gap by presenting a comprehensive generalization analysis for deep ReLU networks trained using gradient descent (GD) and stochastic gradient descent (SGD). Specifically, we establish the first known minimax-optimal rates of excess population risk for both GD and SGD with deep ReLU networks, under the assumption that the network width scales polynomially with respect to the network depth and training sample size. Our results demonstrate that with sufficient width, gradient descent methods for deep ReLU networks can achieve optimal generalization rates on par with kernel methods.

</details>


### [151] [Generalization in Deep Neural Networks: Minimax Rates for Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06772)
*Junyu Zhou,Puyu Wang,Yunwen Lei,Marius Kloft,Yiming Ying*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了深度神经网络（DNN）与核方法在梯度训练下的学习动力学联系，并证明了在过参数化条件下，使用梯度下降（GD）和随机梯度下降（SGD）训练的DNN能够达到极小化最优的泛化误差率，其泛化性能可与核方法相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络的泛化性能是深度学习理论的核心问题，现有研究多集中于浅层架构（如NTK理论），而深层神经网络在回归任务中的统计泛化性质仍不清晰，本文旨在弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 建立了使用梯度方法训练的、带光滑激活函数的DNN与核方法之间的学习动力学联系；2) 基于此联系及核方法的最优性，推导了GD和SGD下超额总体风险的极小化最优收敛速率；3) 理论分析假设网络宽度与样本规模呈多项式关系。

Result: 首次推导出过参数化DNN在GD和SGD训练下的极小化最优泛化误差率，证明在足够宽度下DNN可达到与核方法相当的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 过参数化DNN通过梯度方法训练能够完全继承核方法的学习动力学优势，在泛化性能上实现最优，为深度学习的泛化理论提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Understanding the generalization performance of over-parameterized neural networks has become a central topic in deep learning theory. While recent advances, particularly works under the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regime, have shed light on the behavior of shallow architectures, the statistical generalization properties of deep neural networks (DNNs), especially in regression tasks, remain far less understood. In this paper, we make significant progress toward closing this gap by providing a comprehensive generalization analysis of DNNs trained using gradient-based methods. First, we establish, for the first time, a crucial connection between the learning dynamics of a DNN with smooth activation functions trained via gradient-based methods and those of kernel methods, showing that gradient-based methods on over-parameterized DNNs can fully inherit the favorable learning dynamics of their kernel counterparts. Building on this connection and the well-established optimality of kernel methods, we derive the first known minimax-optimal rates for the excess population risk of both gradient descent (GD) and stochastic gradient descent (SGD), under the assumption that network width scales polynomially with the sample size. Our results demonstrate that, with sufficient width, DNNs trained by GD or SGD can achieve generalization performance comparable to kernel-based methods.

</details>


### [152] [Empirical Transfer Operators and Finite-Sample Change Detection for Noisy Expanding Interval Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06785)
*Aparna Rajput*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维含噪动力系统的有限样本变化检测问题，通过基于划分的经验近似方法估计平稳行为，利用正则化转移矩阵计算经验平稳分布，并基于L1距离构造检测统计量，在有限样本下给出了虚警保证和检测条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变化检测方法在处理含噪动力系统时缺乏有限样本保证，尤其是基于平稳分布的变化检测缺乏理论上的样本量界和误报率控制。

Method: 将状态空间划分后估计有限转移矩阵，施加Doeblin型正则化确保唯一平稳分布；从参考段计算基准经验平稳分布，对每个滑动窗口计算L1距离作为检测得分；推导了经验平稳密度的有限样本界，分离了采样误差、正则化偏差、划分近似误差和噪声偏差。

Result: 得到了单窗口虚警保证，以及在不变密度变化超过估计误差时的充分检测条件；在含噪beta-map合成数据上验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 基于划分的经验平稳分布L1距离统计量可检测含噪动力系统中平稳密度或平稳律的变化，但不能检测所有可能的转移动态变化；理论上的有限样本保证为实际应用提供了可靠性依据。

Abstract: We study finite-sample change detection for one-dimensional noisy dynamical systems using partition-based empirical approximations of stationary behaviour. Given observations from an interval-valued process, we partition the state space, estimate a finite transition matrix from observed transitions between partition elements, and apply a small Doeblin-type regularisation to ensure a unique stationary distribution.
  From an initial reference segment, we compute a baseline empirical stationary distribution \(\widehatπ_{0,ρ}\). For each later sliding window, we compute \(\widehatπ_{t,ρ}\) and define the score \[ S_t=\|\widehatπ_{t,ρ}-\widehatπ_{0,ρ}\|_1. \] Large values of \(S_t\) indicate a change in stationary behaviour relative to the baseline. The statistic detects changes in invariant density or stationary law, but not all possible changes in transition dynamics.
  Under explicit assumptions on empirical transition concentration, finite-state stationary distribution stability, partition approximation, regularisation bias, and noise stability, we derive a finite-sample bound for the empirical stationary density. The bound separates sampling error, regularisation bias, partition approximation error, and noise bias. We then obtain a single-window false-alarm guarantee and a sufficient detection condition when the invariant density changes by more than the estimation error. We illustrate the method on synthetic noisy beta-map change-point experiments.

</details>


### [153] [The Effect of Training Task Diversity on In-Context Learning through the Lens of Low-Dimensional Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06814)
*Soo Min Kwon,Alec S. Xu,Can Yaras,Dogyoon Song,Laura Balzano,Qing Qu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个最小分析模型，将训练任务向量建模为低秩高斯混合，证明了任务多样性（以子空间非重叠列数定义）能提升线性注意力ICL的泛化能力和优化轨迹，并解释了训练任务多样性缩短ICL平台期和实现分布外泛化的现象，最后通过非线性实验验证了结论的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于训练任务多样性对ICL影响的研究中，许多基于函数类定义下的现象缺乏理论解释，本文旨在提供一个可解释的理论框架来统一这些观察。

Method: 将训练任务向量建模为低秩高斯混合分布，通过子空间协方差矩阵的非重叠列数定义任务多样性，在线性注意力机制下进行理论分析，并扩展到非线性Transformer和非线性函数类进行实证验证。

Result: 模型成功解释了：(i) 任务多样性如何缩短ICL的训练平台期；(ii) ICL为何能实现分布外泛化。非线性实验表明结论具有普适性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个可处理的理论框架，统一了现有关于任务多样性对ICL影响的多个观察，为理解ICL机制提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The transformer's emergent ability to perform in-context learning (ICL) has sparked a wide range of studies designed to understand its underlying mechanisms. Existing works often study how training task diversity, defined either as the number of ICL training task vectors or as the number of function classes from which the task vectors are drawn, shapes both the learning dynamics and generalization capabilities of ICL. While both definitions have uncovered many interesting phenomena, many observations under the latter definition remain theoretically unexplained. This paper presents a minimal analytical model under which these phenomena provably emerge from the properties of the training data. By modeling the training task vectors as a mixture of low-rank Gaussians, we show how training task diversity, defined by the number of non-overlapping columns between subspaces that parameterize the covariance matrices, improves both the generalization and optimization trajectory of ICL with linear attention. In particular, we show that our model can explain (i) why training with task diversity shortens the ICL plateau and (ii) why ICL appears to achieve out-of-distribution generalization. We conclude by empirically demonstrating how our results extend to nonlinear transformers and nonlinear function classes. Overall, our work presents a tractable framework to unify existing observations.

</details>


### [154] [Stability beyond Bounded Differences: Sharp Generalization Bounds under Finite $L_p$ Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06855)
*Qianqian Lei,Soham Bonnerjee,Yuefeng Han,Wei Biao Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于L_p稳定性的学习算法泛化分析框架，仅需有限L_p矩条件而非传统的有界性或轻尾假设，通过扩展McDiarmid不等式得到锐利的高概率泛化界，适用于ERM、转导回归和元学习等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法稳定性分析中，高概率泛化保证通常依赖有界性或次高斯/次韦布尔尾部假设，这些条件在重尾或无界损失的现代场景中过于严格。本文旨在放松这些假设，仅需更弱的L_p矩条件即可建立稳定性与泛化之间的联系。

Method: 1) 在L_p矩约束下推导独立随机变量函数的锐利集中不等式，扩展了McDiarmid有界差分技术；2) 利用该集中不等式，结合稳定性概念，推导高概率泛化界；3) 将分析框架应用于经验风险最小化、转导回归和元学习等多种学习范式。

Result: 在仅需有限L_p矩条件下，获得了锐利的高概率泛化保证，表明L_p稳定性足以在有界性不成立时实现鲁棒泛化，显著削弱了稳定性文献中的标准假设。

Conclusion: L_p稳定性是一种比传统有界性更弱的条件，但仍能保证学习算法的鲁棒泛化，为处理重尾或无界损失场景提供了理论基础。

Abstract: While algorithmic stability is a central tool for understanding generalization of learning algorithms, existing high-probability guarantees typically rely on uniform boundedness or sub-Gaussian/sub-Weibull tail assumptions, which can be overly restrictive for modern settings with heavy-tailed or unbounded losses. We develop a stability-based framework that requires only a finite $L_p$ moment condition. Our first contribution is sharp concentration inequalities for functions of independent random variables under $L_p$ constraints, extending McDiarmid's bounded-differences techniques beyond the classical regime. Leveraging these results, we derive sharp high-probability generalization bounds across a range of learning paradigms, including empirical risk minimization, transductive regression, and meta-learning. These guarantees show that $L_p$ stability suffices for robust generalization even when boundedness fails, substantially weakening the standard assumptions in the stability literature.

</details>


### [155] [Deep Single-Index Fréchet Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06957)
*Muqing Cui,Yidong Zhou,Su I Iao,Hans-Georg Müller*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了DeSI（深度单指标Fréchet回归），一种半参数回归框架，用于处理非欧几里得空间输出的回归问题，通过深度神经网络估计可解释的指标方向，并沿一维指标进行Fréchet回归，缓解了维度诅咒并保持了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非欧几里得空间输出（如概率分布、网络、对称正定矩阵）的回归预测在现代数据分析中越来越重要，特别是在输入高维的情况下，需要一种既能缓解维度诅咒又保持可解释性的方法。

Method: DeSI框架假设条件Fréchet均值的单指标结构，利用深度神经网络估计可解释的指标方向以量化输入变量的相对重要性，然后沿得到的一维指标在目标度量空间中进行Fréchet回归。

Result: 在分布、网络和对称正定矩阵上的模拟实验以及新泽西州成分情绪数据的应用中，展示了DeSI的强预测性能。

Conclusion: DeSI通过半参数单指标结构同时缓解了维度诅咒并保持了可解释性，与标准深度神经网络形成对比，并给出了包括一致逼近和收敛速度在内的理论保证。

Abstract: Predicting outputs that are located in non-Euclidean spaces, such as probability distributions, networks, and symmetric positive-definite matrices, is becoming increasingly important in modern data analysis, particularly when inputs are high-dimensional. We propose DeSI (Deep Single-Index Fréchet Regression), a semiparametric framework for regression with metric space-valued outputs and multivariate inputs that assumes a single-index structure for the conditional Fréchet mean. DeSI estimates an interpretable index direction, which quantifies the relative importance of inputs, using a deep neural network, and performs Fréchet regression along the resulting one-dimensional index in the target metric space. This structure mitigates the curse of dimensionality while retaining interpretability, which stands in contrast to standard deep neural networks. We establish theoretical guarantees for DeSI, including uniform approximation and convergence rates, and demonstrate its strong predictive performance through simulations on distributions, networks, and symmetric positive-definite matrices, as well as an application to compositional mood data from New Jersey.

</details>


### [156] [Automatic, Debiased, and Invariant Counterfactual Generation under General Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07399)
*Raphael C Kim,Jingsen Zhu,Ramin Zabih,Michele Santacatterina*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ADIGen是一个用于生成反事实结果的框架，结合Riesz回归、因果不变性和正交统计学习，实现了对高维干预和结果的稳定、去偏且具有不变性的反事实生成，并提供了过剩风险界以证明其在一般干预下控制反事实风险的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实生成模型存在估计不稳定、跨环境泛化能力差以及受 nuisance 模型误设影响而产生偏差等问题，亟需一种能够解决这些局限性的鲁棒框架。

Method: ADIGen框架融合了三项关键技术：(1) Riesz回归，避免不稳定的密度比估计；(2) 因果不变性，提升分布偏移下的泛化能力；(3) 正交统计学习，提供针对nuisance模型误设的双重稳健保证。

Result: 提供了过剩风险界，证明ADIGen在一般干预下能控制反事实风险，其中包含乘积偏差nuisance余项以及跨环境的不变风险界。

Conclusion: ADIGen能够有效支持复杂干预下的决策，在稳定性、泛化性和鲁棒性方面优于现有方法。

Abstract: Generative models for counterfactual outcomes have great potential to support decision-making under complex interventions, but existing approaches are limited by unstable estimation, poor generalization across environments, and bias from nuisance model misspecification. We introduce ADIGen, a framework for automatic, debiased, and invariant counterfactual generation under general interventions, including high-dimensional interventions and outcomes. ADIGen combines Riesz regression to avoid unstable density-ratio estimation, causal invariance to improve generalization under distribution shift, and orthogonal statistical learning to obtain doubly robust guarantees against nuisance model misspecification. We provide excess-risk bounds showing that ADIGen controls counterfactual risk under general interventions, with a product-bias nuisance remainder and an invariant risk bound across environments.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Smooth Hard-Thresholding for Singular Values with Stein's Unbiased Risk Estimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06632)
*Guanzhong Yang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对低秩矩阵去噪中经典SVD截断方法缺乏有限样本风险原则的问题，提出了一种基于Stein无偏风险估计（SURE）的数学严谨替代方案。通过引入Logistic光滑硬阈值谱估计器，证明了其满足Stein引理的正则条件，并推导出无偏固定阈值风险估计。对于有限候选阈值，光滑SURE目标函数的排序最终与一个简单极限分数一致，该极限分数具有与有偏硬阈值SURE公式相同的代数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SVD图像去噪方法通过匹配残差奇异值能量与估计噪声能量选择截断秩，但这种规则并非有限样本风险原则，因为拟合的低秩近似不可避免地吸收了部分噪声。本文旨在开发一种数学上严谨的替代方法。

Method: 引入Logistic光滑硬阈值谱估计器替代不连续的奇异值硬阈值，使其满足谱估计器版本Stein引理的正则条件。推导出在高斯噪声下精确无偏的固定阈值风险估计。对于有限候选阈值，证明光滑SURE目标函数的排序最终与一个简单极限分数一致。

Result: 光滑SURE目标函数的排序与一个简单极限分数一致，该极限分数具有与有偏硬阈值SURE公式相同的代数形式，但仅用作排序有限候选阈值的计算工具。

Conclusion: 选择最小化阈值是数据自适应调参步骤；所选SURE值不应被解释为最终所选估计量的无偏风险估计。

Abstract: Low-rank matrix denoising is a central primitive in patch-based image restoration and many other inverse problems. Classical SVD-based image denoising methods often choose a truncation rank by matching residual singular-value energy with an estimated noise energy, but this rule is not a finite-sample risk principle because a fitted low-rank approximation inevitably absorbs part of the noise. This paper develops a mathematically rigorous alternative based on Stein's unbiased risk estimate (SURE). Since singular value hard thresholding is discontinuous and does not satisfy the hypotheses of Stein's lemma, we introduce a logistic smooth hard-threshold spectral estimator. We prove that the smooth shrinker satisfies the regularity conditions required by a spectral-estimator version of Stein's lemma, and therefore admits an exactly unbiased fixed-threshold risk estimate under Gaussian noise. For a fixed observed matrix and a finite set of candidate thresholds separated from the observed singular values, the ordering of the fixed-threshold smooth SURE objective eventually agrees with a simple limiting score. The limiting score has the same algebraic form as the biased hard-threshold SURE formula, but here it is used only as a computational device for ranking finite candidates. Selecting the minimizing threshold is a data-adaptive tuning step; the selected SURE value should not be interpreted as an unbiased risk estimate of the finally selected estimator.

</details>


### [158] [Sequential testing of conditionally constrained hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06769)
*Eugenio Clerico*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了条件非参数假设检验中e过程的特征，证明每个e过程都被可预测的仿射单步e变量乘积逐点控制，从而可将任意e过程替换为测试超鞅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将完全类定理从单步约束检验和有界一维条件均值检验推广到更广泛的条件序贯设定中，为条件非参数假设的序贯检验提供理论基础。

Method: 通过特征化有限个条件约束定义的条件非参数假设的e过程完整类，证明每个e过程都被可预测的仿射单步e变量乘积逐点支配。

Result: 得到了完全类定理：每个e过程都可被可预测的仿射单步e变量乘积逐点控制，因此任意e过程可无损失地替换为测试超鞅。

Conclusion: 将完全类结果从单步和有界一维情形推广到条件序贯设定，为条件非参数假设检验提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We explicitly characterise the full class of e-processes for testing conditional non-parametric hypotheses, defined by finitely many conditional constraints. Our main result is a complete-class theorem: every e-process for such a hypothesis is point-wise dominated by a predictable product of affine one-step e-variables. Therefore, for a broad class of conditional testing problems, arbitrary e-processes can be replaced without loss by test supermartingales. This extends previous complete-class results from single-step constrained testing and bounded one-dimensional conditional mean testing to a broader conditional sequential setting.

</details>


### [159] [Integral stochastic orders of $m$-generalized order statistics from transform-ordered nonparametric families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07022)
*Idir Arab,Tommaso Lando,Paulo Eduardo Oliveira,Tomasz Rychlik*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了m-广义顺序统计量在递增凹序、递增凸序和星形随机序下的比较条件，提供了基于随机变换序的非参数方法，涵盖多种与广义帕累托分布相关的分布类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为m-广义顺序统计量在几种重要的随机序（递增凹序、递增凸序、星形序）下的比较提供充分条件，从而能够对经典顺序统计量、删失II型顺序统计量和记录值进行排序。

Method: 采用非参数方法，假设数据满足某种随机变换序性质（即适当的形状条件），而非指定具体的参数形式。该框架涵盖了通过变换序与广义帕累托分布及负广义帕累托分布相关的多种分布类。

Result: 导出了比较m-广义顺序统计量的充分条件，这些条件依赖于广义顺序统计量的参数和底层分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的条件能够对经典顺序统计量、删失II型顺序统计量和记录值进行排序，且框架具有普适性，涵盖了许多与广义帕累托分布相关的分布类。

Abstract: We provide sufficient conditions for comparing $m$-generalized order statistics with respect to the increasing concave, increasing convex, and star-shaped stochastic orders. These conditions allow us to rank classical order statistics, selected censored type-II order statistics, and records. They depend on both the parameters of the generalized order statistics and the underlying distribution. Rather than assuming a specific parametric form, we adopt a nonparametric approach and assume some stochastic transform-ordered property, that is, some suitable shape condition. This framework encompasses many relevant classes of distributions that are related, via transform order, to the generalized and the negative generalized Pareto distribution.

</details>


### [160] [Ising Models on Inhomogeneous Random Graphs: Inference, Local Asymptotic Minimaxity, and Limit of Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07065)
*Somabha Mukherjee,Sanchayan Bhowal,Anirban Chatterjee,Bhaswar B. Bhattacharya*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对次临界参数区域中非齐次随机图上的Ising模型，建立了具有渐近最优性保证的推断框架。提出了计算高效且与MLE渐近等价的闭合形式估计量，并证明了其达到局部渐近极小极大最优风险，同时推导了似然比检验的局部势和极小极大检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非齐次随机图上的Ising模型中，最大似然估计虽然在理论上最优但计算不可行，且现有工作缺乏尖锐的渐近最优性保证。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，提供兼具计算效率和统计最优性的推断方法。

Method: (1) 刻画了单样本下MLE的自然参数渐近分布，覆盖稀疏和稠密网络；(2) 提出基于似然方程一步近似的闭合形式估计量；(3) 建立Hájek-Le Cam型局部渐近极小极大定理；(4) 推导似然比检验的局部势和极小极大检测率；(5) 建立了充分统计量（Hamiltonian）与随机配分函数的新波动结果。

Result: 所提出的闭合形式估计量与MLE具有相同的渐近分布和方差，并在真值参数的收缩邻域上达到最小的渐近最大风险（包括速率和领先常数）。导出了对应的实验极限。建立了Goodness-of-fit检验的局部势和极小极大检测率。

Conclusion: 这是网络依赖数据中首批获得尖锐渐近最优性结果的工作之一。所提出的框架兼具计算可行性和统计最优性，为次临界Ising模型的推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an inferential framework with sharp asymptotic optimality guarantees for Ising models on inhomogeneous random graphs in the subcritical parameter regime. We begin by characterizing the asymptotic distribution of the maximum likelihood (ML) estimate of the natural parameter, based on a single sample from the underlying model, covering both sparse and dense network regimes. Next, to overcome the computational intractability of the ML method, we propose a simple closed-form estimate obtained from a one-step approximation to the likelihood equation. We show that this estimate attains the same asymptotic distribution and variance as the ML estimate, thereby yielding a computationally efficient and asymptotically valid confidence interval for the natural parameter. We complement these inferential results by establishing a Hájek--Le Cam-type local asymptotic minimax theorem, showing that the proposed estimate achieves the smallest possible asymptotic maximum risk, both in rate and in leading constant, over shrinking neighborhoods of the true parameter. We also derive the corresponding limit of experiments. To the best of our knowledge, these are among the first sharp asymptotic optimality results for network-dependent data. Finally, we study goodness-of-fit testing for the natural parameter, deriving the local power of the likelihood ratio test and minimax detection rates. Our analysis relies on new fluctuation results for the sufficient statistic (Hamiltonian) and for the random partition function of Ising models on inhomogeneous random graphs, which are of independent interest.

</details>


### [161] [The Balance Property: The Constrained Case, with a View on Risk Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07276)
*Mario V. Wüthrich*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文讨论了保险定价中统计模型的平衡性质，提出了一种优于现有两种方法的第三种平衡校正方法——约束GLM拟合，并揭示了平衡性质与事后风险分担规则之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险定价中拟合统计模型的平衡性质（确保总精算价格等于总观测损失）至关重要，但现有平衡校正方法存在不足，需要更优的解决方案。

Method: 提出约束GLM拟合（constrained GLM fitting）作为第三种平衡校正方法，并与Lindholm-Wüthrich（2026）讨论的两种流行方法进行比较。

Result: 约束GLM拟合方法在平衡校正效果上优于先前讨论的两种方法。

Conclusion: 约束GLM拟合是一种更优的平衡校正方法，且平衡性质与事后风险分担规则存在内在关联。

Abstract: The balance property is an important property of fitted statistical models deployed for insurance pricing. It guarantees that the total actuarial price in the fitted model is equal to the totally observed loss used to fit the model. This can be seen as an in-sample global unbiasedness property. Maximum likelihood fitted generalized linear models (GLMs) with canonical links automatically fulfill the balance property. Lindholm-Wüthrich (Scandinavian Actuarial Journal, 2026) discussed two popular balance correction methods in case the balance property fails to hold. This note extends this discussion with a third method, constrained GLM fitting, that turns out to be superior over the two previously discussed ones. Moreover, we highlight the connection between the balance property and ex-post risk sharing rules.

</details>


### [162] [A Temporal Spatial Minimax Rate for Smoothly-Varying Distributions in Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07325)
*Munsik Kim*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在2-Wasserstein空间中，从有限个含噪历史快照估计曲线未来值$μ_{t_n+h}$的极小化极大速率，在速度场的k阶协变导数满足绝热界$\|\nabla_t^k v\|\le\varepsilon$条件下，得到了统一的时空极小化下界，指数为$γ_d(k+1)/(k+1+γ_d)$，其中$γ_d=\min(1/d,1/2)$。该下界插值了与维数无关的外推误差$\varepsilon h^{k+1}$和空间估计速率$M^{-γ_d}$。文章建立了设计依赖形式的下界，在等间距设计下得到闭式指数，并在$k=0$（$d\ge3$）以及平移子模型下证明了匹配上界，数值实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在水斯曼空间中从历史观测数据估计动态曲线未来值，是融合时间序列分析与最优传输理论的基础性问题，具有重要的理论意义和实际应用价值。现有工作大多关注静态分布估计或确定性动态曲线的重建，缺乏对随机噪声和有限观测下动态预测的极小化极大理论刻画，特别是时间维度和空间维度估计误差的相互作用机制尚不清晰。

Method: 采用"时间-空间缩减"方法：将时间轴上的光滑度预算定义出一个可达的Wasserstein球，将运输打包嵌入时间轴；利用Fano引理控制整个快照实验的信息量，空间打包采用经典构造，但光滑性允许的时间嵌入和全窗口分析是新的。下界以设计依赖形式（含设计加权有效样本量）给出，对任意观测时间有效，在等间距设计下得到闭式指数。

Result: 得到了统一的时空极小化下界：在规则、局部传输丰富的子类上，每个估计量在$W_2$风险下都有M指数$γ_d(k+1)/(k+1+γ_d)$，其中$γ_d=\min(1/d,1/2)$。该下界插值了维数无关的外推误差$\varepsilon h^{k+1}$（即使精确已知过去也不可避免的未来预测成本）和空间估计速率$M^{-γ_d}$，当$k\to\infty$时恢复静态分布估计速率。在$k=0$（$d\ge3$）和平移子模型（对所有$k$）下建立了匹配上界。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了Wasserstein空间中动态预测的时空误差权衡机制：时间外推误差由绝热界控制且与维数无关，空间估计误差随维数诅咒，两者通过"光滑度预算"非线性交互。对于$k\ge1$的一般情况，协变估计量条件地达到了速率（依赖于比较几何偏差界和最优传输图估计速率），但无条件的一般$k$上界仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We study the minimax rate of estimating a future value $μ_{t_n+h}$ of a curve $t\mapstoμ_t$ in the $2$-Wasserstein space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d)$ from finitely many noisy snapshots of its past, under an adiabatic bound $\|\nabla_t^k v\|\le\varepsilon$ on the $k$-th covariant derivative of the velocity field. Our central result is a unified temporal-spatial minimax lower bound: over regular, locally transport-rich subclasses, every estimator incurs $W_2$-risk with $M$-exponent $γ_d(k+1)/(k+1+γ_d)$, $γ_d=\min(1/d,1/2)$ ($M$ the total sample size). It follows from a temporal-to-spatial reduction: the smoothness budget defines a reachable $W_2$-ball into which a transport packing is embedded along the time axis, and the information of the entire snapshot experiment is controlled by a Fano argument -- the spatial packing is classical, but its smoothness-admissible temporal embedding and the full-window analysis are new. The bound interpolates a dimension-free extrapolation floor of order $\varepsilon h^{k+1}$ -- the irreducible cost of an unobserved future, present even with the exact past -- and the spatial estimation curse $M^{-γ_d}$, recovering the static distribution-estimation rate as $k\to\infty$. We state the lower bound in a design-dependent form -- with a design-weighted effective sample size -- valid for arbitrary observation times, and obtain the closed-form exponent in the dense (equispaced) regime. The matching upper bound is established at $k=0$ (rate $M^{-1/(d+1)}$, $d\ge3$) and, in a translation submodel, for all $k$; for $k\ge1$ a covariant estimator attains the rate conditionally on two estimates (a comparison-geometry bias bound and an optimal-transport map-estimation rate), leaving the unconditional general-$k$ upper bound as an open problem. Numerical experiments on synthetic curved and flat families corroborate the predicted exponents.

</details>


### [163] [Dependence Measures via Adapted Optimal Transport: Stability and Rates of Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07354)
*Jonathan Ansari,Johannes Wiesel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出基于最优传输的收敛模式，恢复条件分布弱收敛的连续性，并建立与适应Wasserstein距离、Knothe-Rosenblatt距离及copula上d1度量的联系。基于适应经验测度提出copula估计器，并与经典棋盘估计器对比，两者均获得O(N^{-1/3})收敛率，进而得到多类依赖度量（包括基于秩和重排的）的相同收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖度量（如Chatterjee秩相关）能刻画独立性和完美函数依赖，但缺乏弱连续性，导致基于经验分布的经典插件估计器适用性受限，因其通过条件分布而非联合分布定义。

Method: 引入基于最优传输的收敛模式，恢复条件分布弱收敛的连续性；建立该模式与适应Wasserstein距离、Knothe-Rosenblatt距离及copula上d1度量的关系；基于适应经验测度提出copula估计器。

Result: 所提copula估计器与经典棋盘估计器均在相关度量下获得O(N^{-1/3})收敛率；该收敛率可推广至基于秩和重排的依赖度量插件估计器。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输框架有效解决了依赖度量缺乏弱连续性的根本问题，为条件分布相关依赖度量的估计提供了统一的理论保证。

Abstract: Recently studied dependence measures, such as Chatterjee's rank correlation, that characterize both independence and perfect functional dependence, provide a powerful framework for detecting nonlinear dependencies. However, these measures cannot be weakly continuous, which limits the applicability of classical plug-in estimators based on empirical distributions. This obstruction is natural, as such measures are defined via conditional distributions and not through their joint law alone. In this paper, we introduce an optimal transport-based mode of convergence that captures weak convergence of conditional distributions and restores continuity for a broad class of dependence measures. We relate this mode of convergence to the adapted Wasserstein distance, the Knothe-Rosenblatt distance and the d1-metric on copulas. Building on this perspective, we propose a copula estimator based on the adapted empirical measure and compare it with the classical rank-based checkerboard estimator. For both estimators, we derive O(N^{-1/3})-rates of convergence with respect to metrics that capture conditional weak continuity. As a consequence, we obtain the same rates for plug-in estimators of several classes of dependence measures, including rank-based and rearranged dependence measures.

</details>


### [164] [Non-asymptotic bounds for quasi-MLE, misspecified models, and dependence under group sequential sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07499)
*Julian Aronowitz,Jay Bartroff*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文推导了在模型可能误设和组内相关条件下，分组序列拟最大似然估计量的渐近多元正态极限和显式非渐近正态近似界。通过Stein方法得到的界具有已知常数，适用于似然函数可能与真实数据生成机制不同的依赖数据估计问题。研究在带有随机组效应的泊松广义线性混合模型中计算了极限协方差结构和有限样本界，并使用癫痫临床试验数据进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分组序列估计理论大多假设模型正确设定且观测独立，而实际临床和纵向数据中常存在模型误设和组内相关性。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，为更现实的统计推断场景提供理论基础。

Method: 采用Stein方法推导非渐近正态近似界，获得具有已知常数的显式界；通过泊松广义线性混合模型（含随机组效应）具体计算极限协方差结构和有限样本界；利用癫痫临床试验数据验证结果。

Result: 成功推导了模型误设和组内相关条件下分组序列QMLE的渐近多元正态极限，给出了显式的非渐近正态近似界，并在泊松GLMM中得到了具体的极限协方差和有限样本界表达。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架扩展了分组序列估计的适用范围，允许模型误设和组内依赖，为更广泛的依赖数据估计问题提供了理论保障和实际可用的误差界。

Abstract: We derive asymptotic multivariate normal limits and explicit non-asymptotic normal approximation bounds for group sequential quasi-maximum likelihood estimators under possible model misspecification and within-group dependence. The bounds, obtained using Stein's method, have known constants and apply to a class of dependent-data estimating problems in which the likelihood used for estimation may differ from the true data-generating mechanism. We compute the limiting covariance structure and finite-sample bound explicitly for a Poisson generalized linear mixed model with random group effects and illustrate the results using data from an epilepsy clinical trial.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Elmes*: Automated Construction of Fine-Grained Evaluation Rubrics for Large Language Models in Long-Tail Educational Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06546)
*Tao Liu,Ye Lu,Ruohua Zhang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Aimin Zhou,Hao Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Elmes*框架，用于构建教育场景下细粒度的评估标准，并构建了Edu-330基准（330个场景、11000+指标），实验发现LLM在教育能力上是多维的（知识强不等于教学能力强），且LLM裁判存在自偏好等偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估基准要么关注领域通用正确性，要么依赖人工设计的评估标准，难以扩展到长尾教学场景。

Method: 提出Elmes*框架，包含声明式多智能体引擎（教师-学生-裁判交互）和SceneGen自演化模块（协同优化评估标准与测试数据）；构建Edu-330基准（11学科、3年级段、10任务类型、330场景、11000+二级指标）。

Result: 顶尖LLM的差异主要体现在创造力和价值观整合；知识强的模型在苏格拉底式支架教学上可能失败；教育专用模型InnoSpark获得最佳人工评估平均分；LLM裁判能保持与人工相当的排名但方差更小，但存在自偏好等偏见；专家打分的few-shot锚定可改善人-LLM对齐。

Conclusion: Elmes*为教育学扎根的LLM评估提供了可扩展的诊断基础设施，揭示了教育能力的多维性。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for education requires measuring how models teach, not only what they know. Existing benchmarks emphasize domain-general correctness or depend on manually designed rubrics that scale poorly to long-tail pedagogical scenarios. We introduce Elmes*, an end-to-end framework for constructing, refining, and applying fine-grained scenario-specific rubrics. Elmes* combines a declarative multi-agent engine for teacher--student--judge interactions with SceneGen, a self-evolving module that co-optimizes evaluation criteria and test data from expert-defined pedagogical dimensions. Using Elmes*, we build Edu-330, covering 330 scenarios across 11 subjects, 3 grade bands, and 10 task types, with over 1{,}000 second-level indicators. Experiments on Edu-330 and four expert-authored gold-standard scenarios show that educational capability is multidimensional: top-tier LLMs differ mainly in creativity and values integration, knowledge-strong models may fail at Socratic scaffolding, and the education-specialized InnoSpark achieves the best human-evaluated average score. LLM judges preserve human-comparable rankings with much lower scoring variance, but exhibit judge-specific biases such as self-preference. Ablations show that expert-scored few-shot anchoring improves human--LLM alignment, while reasoning enforcement and greedy decoding are model-dependent. Elmes* thus provides scalable diagnostic infrastructure for pedagogically grounded LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [166] [FAIR-Calib: Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration for Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06547)
*Haoyu Huang,Linlin Yang,Sheng Xu,Boyu Liu,Guodong Guo,Zhongqian Fu,Hang Zhou,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAIR-Calib，一种面向扩散大语言模型的后训练量化框架，通过两阶段校准策略保护写前沿的不稳定决策，显著减少量化导致的决策翻转和性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在迭代精炼令牌时存在"稳定性滞后"问题——早期决策即使被写入后仍然脆弱。后训练量化（PTQ）误差容易翻转写前沿处的边界决策，这些错误被永久锁定并放大，导致性能严重下降。

Method: FAIR-Calib包含两阶段：（1）阶段I：在全精度教师模型上探测，估计结合前沿命中率和掩码阶段可靠性的位置先验；（2）阶段II：进行离策略、逐层校准，通过最小化重加权后的隐藏状态MSE，优先保护脆弱的前沿状态，无需昂贵的端到端扩散 rollout。理论上证明了加权目标是输出KL散度的代理。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型上（W4A4设置），FAIR-Calib一致优于现有基线方法，显著减少了前沿决策翻转，并在多种基准测试中抑制了提交后不匹配问题。

Conclusion: FAIR-Calib通过感知前沿不稳定性的重加权校准，有效解决了dLLMs在量化过程中边界决策被锁定放大的问题，提供了一种高效且理论上合理的PTQ方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) refine tokens iteratively but commit them irreversibly, leading to a "stability lag" where early decisions remain fragile even after being written. We reveal that Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) error easily flips these borderline decisions at the write frontier, which are then permanently locked in and amplified. To address this, we propose Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration (FAIR-Calib), a two-stage PTQ framework for dLLMs. Stage I probes a full-precision teacher to estimate a position prior that combines frontier hits and masked-stage reliability. Stage II performs off-policy, layer-wise calibration by minimizing a reweighted hidden-state MSE, effectively prioritizing the protection of fragile frontier states without requiring expensive end-to-end diffusion rollouts. We further theoretically justify our weighted objective as a surrogate for output KL divergence. Empirically, FAIR-Calib consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on LLaDA and Dream (W4A4), significantly reducing frontier decision flips and suppressing post-commit mismatches across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [167] [Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network for Polymer Classification using THz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06554)
*Roshni Mahtani,Ilán Carretero,Laura Monroy,Aldo Moreno-Oyervides,Oscar Elías Bonilla-Manrique,Rocío del Amor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），结合太赫兹双梳光谱技术（THz-DCS）对12种聚合物（包括纯聚合物、多层薄膜、商业共混物和生物聚合物）进行分类，达到85.2%的准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚合物识别方法难以实现可靠的分类，需要一种快速、高分辨率、无损的替代方案来确保回收塑料的质量与安全性。

Method: 采用THz-DCS获取光谱数据，设计MSFAN深度学习架构，包含特征门控信号校准、多尺度并行卷积捕获频率模式、跨特征注意力与注意力池化机制，以突出最具信息量的太赫兹区域。

Result: MSFAN在12类聚合物分类任务中达到85.2%的准确率，持续优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: THz-DCS结合深度学习技术可有效、可扩展且可解释地实现聚合物分类，为回收塑料质量保障提供有力工具。

Abstract: Reliable polymer identification is essential for ensuring the quality and safety of recycled plastics, yet conventional sorting and spectroscopic techniques often struggle to deliver robust discrimination. Terahertz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy (THz-DCS) offers a promising alternative, providing rapid, high-resolution, and non-destructive measurements. In this work, we leverage THz-DCS to classify 12 types of polymers, including pure polymers, multilayer films, commercial blends, and biopolymers. To handle the complexity of these spectral signals, we propose the Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network (MSFAN), a novel deep learning architecture tailored for THz-DCS data. The framework integrates feature gating for signal recalibration and multi-scale parallel convolutions to capture diverse frequency patterns. These features are further refined through cross-feature attention and attention pooling, enabling the model to intrinsically highlight the most informative THz regions. MSFAN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, reaching a classification accuracy of 85.2%. This study demonstrates the potential of combining THz-DCS with deep learning techniques for effective, scalable, and interpretable polymer classification.

</details>


### [168] [MacArena: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents on an Online macOS Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06560)
*Victor Muryn,Maksym Shamrai,Sofiia Mazepa,Yehor Khodysko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MacArena是一个针对macOS的GUI智能体基准测试，包含421个任务（覆盖50个应用），发现现有模型在macOS上的表现远不如在Linux基准上，且模型排名在不同平台间会发生逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: macOS在CUAs评估中严重缺失：现有唯一基准macOSWorld仅覆盖少量第一方应用且任务简单，运行在x86虚拟机上不兼容Apple Silicon，无法真实反映macOS特有的GUI挑战。

Method: 构建了包含421个手动验证任务的MacArena基准，整合了OSWorld移植任务、macOSWorld内容和49个全新的macOS原生任务，基于Apple Silicon的Virtualization框架运行。

Result: 模型在现有基准上的强表现反映的是对任务分布的熟悉度而非跨平台GUI能力；排名在移植任务和macOS原生任务之间发生逆转，领先模型在MacArena子集上落后超过26%。

Conclusion: macOS对当前GUI智能体而言是一个本质上更困难的环境，现有基准不足以衡量跨平台的GUI通用能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate graphical user interfaces (GUIs) through vision and control primitives, and their capabilities have advanced rapidly, driven in part by standardized online evaluation benchmarks such as OSWorld, which serve both as evaluation tools and as training environments for reinforcement learning. However, macOS remains underserved in this landscape: the only existing benchmark, macOSWorld, covers a narrow slice of first-party applications with simpler tasks, and runs on x86 virtual machines incompatible with Apple Silicon. We introduce MacArena, a benchmark of 421 manually verified tasks spanning 50 applications that combines a curated port of OSWorld tasks, content sourced from macOSWorld, and 49 new macOS-native tasks, all running on Apple's native Virtualization framework on Apple Silicon. We argue that macOS presents distinct GUI challenges beyond what Linux-based benchmarks capture, and our evaluation supports this claim: strong model performance on existing benchmarks can reflect familiarity with task distributions rather than genuine cross-platform GUI competence. Notably, model rankings invert between ported and macOS-native tasks, with a leading model trailing by over 26% on the MacArena subset, suggesting that macOS poses a genuinely harder environment for current GUI agents.

</details>


### [169] [WAV: Multi-Resolution Block Residual Routing for Deep Decoder-Only Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06564)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAV v1 提出一种轻量级多分辨率残差路由方法，通过为每个Transformer块增加方向性细节基（相位基和分割基）来增强残差路由，在深层（48层）解码器-only模型中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PreNorm残差流使用固定单位权重聚合子层更新，而最近提出的Attention Residuals引入了内容相关的深度路由。但Block Attention Residuals仅存储块内低频总残差位移，丢失了注意力vs MLP不平衡、块早期vs后期动态等方向性结构信息。

Method: WAV v1 为每个Transformer块增加两种方向性细节基：相位基（对比注意力与MLP更新）和分割基（对比早期与后期子层更新）。这些基通过相同的深度softmax混合器与标准块摘要一起路由，并采用负细节源初始化和分离的RMS匹配来稳定训练。

Result: 在字符级TinyStories和Text8语言建模任务上，12层时优势不明显，24层时具有竞争力，48层时显著优于所有基线。48层下，TinyStories验证损失从0.4960降至0.4738，Text8从0.9363降至0.9305，且额外参数可忽略。

Conclusion: 方向性残差细节（而非仅块级和）对于深层Transformer的残差路由扩展至关重要。

Abstract: Residual connections are central to training deep Transformers, but standard PreNorm residual streams aggregate sublayer updates with fixed unit weights. Recent Attention Residuals replace this fixed accumulation with content-dependent depth-wise routing, and Block Attention Residuals make the mechanism efficient by routing over block-level residual summaries. However, a single block summary stores only the low-frequency total residual displacement inside a block, discarding directional structure such as attention-vs-MLP imbalance and early-vs-late block dynamics. We propose WAV v1, a lightweight multi-resolution residual routing method for decoder-only Transformers. Instead of representing each block only by its accumulated residual sum, WAV v1 augments every block with two directional detail bases: a phase basis that contrasts attention and MLP updates, and a split basis that contrasts early and late sublayer updates. These bases are routed together with standard block summaries through the same depth-wise softmax mixer, while negative detail-source initialization and detached RMS matching stabilize training. On character-level TinyStories and Text8 language modeling, WAV v1 shows a clear depth-dependent benefit. Although it is not consistently beneficial at 12 layers, it becomes competitive at 24 layers and outperforms all baselines at 48 layers. At 48 layers, WAV v1 reduces validation loss relative to Block AttnRes from 0.4960 to 0.4738 on TinyStories and from 0.9363 to 0.9305 on Text8, with negligible additional parameters. These results suggest that directional residual details, not only block-level sums, are important for scaling residual routing in deeper Transformers.

</details>


### [170] [Are you sure? A Comprehensive and Comprehensible Survey of Uncertainty Quantification in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06567)
*Julia Reuter,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了符号回归中的不确定性量化问题，将现有研究分为频率派、贝叶斯和模型选择三个方向，指出该领域仍待深入探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归在现实决策中因缺乏不确定性量化支持而受限，而UQ对避免过拟合和辅助决策至关重要，但该问题未被先前文献明确关注。

Method: 通过综述方式，梳理UQ核心概念，并将现有SR中的UQ研究归纳为频率派方法、贝叶斯方法和模型选择方法三大方向。

Result: 识别出SR中的UQ研究仍处于早期阶段，三个方向各有局限，整体缺乏成熟可靠的UQ方法。

Conclusion: 强调SR中的UQ研究亟待加强，呼吁未来针对SR开发更可靠的不确定性量化方法。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is a class of methods that systematically explore the space of mathematical functions to discover models that accurately capture the underlying relationships in a dataset. Despite recent advances in the field, a lack of support for uncertainty quantification (UQ) limits its adoption in real-world decision processes. In regression analysis, UQ provides important information about the model reliability, which can both help to avoid overfitting by accounting for uncertainty in the data, and provide insights for decision-making. This survey is the first to clearly address this issue, with the objective of introducing essential UQ concepts and reviewing the current literature on UQ in SR, which can be broadly organized into three research directions: frequentist, Bayesian, and model selection. Despite its importance, UQ in SR is still underexplored, which motivates further research into reliable UQ methods for SR.

</details>


### [171] [Generative Models Erode Human Temporal Learning Through Market Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06572)
*Wenjun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了当前（亚AGI级别）的生成式模型对知识和文化生产构成结构性风险。作者提出“人类时间学习”（HTL）概念，指通过长期投入问题解决形成的路径依赖型知识积累。生成式输出在表面特征上日益接近HTL密集型工作，导致验证输出是否反映真实人类学习的成本超过预期收益。一旦验证失去经济合理性，评估者将不论生产模式均奖励输出，而投入多年学习的生产者将在价格上与近乎零成本的生成输出竞争。作者称此路径为“价值崩塌”，并通过昂贵检查框架进行形式化。来自学术出版、法律实践、内容平台和软件安全的跨领域证据映射出验证侵蚀的四个阶段。对齐成功是正交因素：更佳对齐的模型缩小了人与AI输出之间的可观察差距，使来源验证更加困难，并加剧了对HTL密集型工作的竞争压力——即使单个AI输出质量有所提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示当前生成式模型（尚未达到AGI水平）对知识生产和文化创造的结构性风险，特别是通过“价值崩塌”机制解释为什么即使AI输出质量提升，人类长期学习投入的成果仍会面临系统性贬值。

Method: 通过昂贵检查框架（costly-inspection framework）进行形式化建模，并结合跨领域证据（学术出版、法律实践、内容平台、软件安全）进行映射分析，将验证侵蚀过程划分为四个阶段。

Result: 跨领域证据一致映射出验证侵蚀的四阶段模式；模型对齐成功与价值崩塌风险正交——更好的对齐反而加剧了验证困难和竞争压力。

Conclusion: 即使生成式模型未达到AGI水平，其当前能力已足以通过“价值崩塌”机制对HTL密集型工作构成结构性威胁；单纯追求模型对齐不足以解决此问题，需要重新设计验证激励机制和知识生产的经济结构。

Abstract: We argue that modern generative models create structural risks for knowledge and cultural production at current, sub-AGI capability levels. We define Human Temporal Learning (HTL) as path-dependent knowledge accumulation through sustained engagement with problems over time. Generative outputs increasingly resemble HTL-intensive work in surface features, so verifying whether a given output reflects genuine human learning grows costly relative to its expected benefit. Once verification loses economic justification, evaluators reward outputs regardless of production mode, and producers who invested years of learning compete on price against outputs that cost almost nothing to generate. We call this pathway value collapse and formalize it through a costly-inspection framework. Cross-domain evidence from academic publishing, legal practice, content platforms, and software security maps onto four stages of verification erosion. Alignment success is orthogonal. Better-aligned models narrow observable gaps between human and AI outputs, making source verification harder and intensifying competitive pressure against HTL-intensive work even when individual AI outputs improve.

</details>


### [172] [Skip a Layer or Loop It? Learning Program-of-Layers in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06574)
*Ziyue Li,Yang Li,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了预训练大语言模型存在一种无需额外训练的灵活动态层程序（PoLar），即可以将预训练层作为模块进行跳过或循环执行，为每个输入定制化执行路径。大多数输入可以用更短的执行程序达到相同或更高精度，并且可以通过替代程序修正原始LLM的错误预测。为此提出了轻量级PoLar预测网络来动态生成执行程序，在数学推理基准上，PoLar一致提升了准确率且通常使用更少的层数，在分布外评估下依然有效，表明固定深度执行仅利用了LLM潜在推理能力的一小部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM按固定深度和顺序逐层执行所有层，这种非循环的静态执行方式可能限制了模型的推理效率与能力。作者希望探索是否存在更灵活的动态层执行机制，以及能否通过跳过或重复某些层来提升推理性能和效率。

Method: 提出PoLar（Program-of-Layers）框架：将预训练层视为可模块化调用的基本单元；训练一个轻量级PoLar预测网络，为每个输入生成动态执行程序，决定哪些层被跳过或重复执行；该预测网络以输入特征为条件，输出层级的执行策略。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，PoLar一致提升了准确率，且通常使用更少的层数；能够修正原始LLM的错误预测；在分布外评估下仍然保持性能增益。

Conclusion: 固定深度执行仅捕获了LLM潜在推理能力的一小部分，LLM存在更丰富的动态层执行空间，通过定制化的程序化层执行可以更高效地释放模型的推理潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform inference by following a fixed depth and order, non-recurrent execution of all layers. We reveal the wide existence of training-free, flexible, dynamic program-of-layers (PoLar), where pretrained layers can be packed as modules and then skipped or looped to form a customized program for each input. For most inputs, substantially shorter program executions can achieve the same or better accuracy, while incorrect predictions of the original LLM can be corrected by alternative programs with fewer layers. These observations indicate that inference admits multiple valid latent computations beyond the standard forward pass. To efficiently achieve PoLar in practice, we propose a lightweight PoLar prediction network, which learns to generate execution programs that dynamically skip or repeat pretrained layers for each input. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PoLar consistently improves accuracy over standard inference and prior dynamic-depth methods, often while executing fewer layers, and that these gains persist under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results suggest that fixed-depth execution captures only a narrow subset of an LLM's latent reasoning capacity.

</details>


### [173] [Gaussian Process Latent Factor Regression for Low-Data, High-Dimensional Output Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06576)
*Edward T. Stevenson,Eric T. Wolf,Mei Ting Mak,N. J. Mayne,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）模型，将压缩与预测耦合于单一目标，实现用高斯过程从少量训练样本预测高维输出，并在系外行星气候模型空间仿真中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多输出高斯过程难以处理高维输出，而主成分分析加高斯过程回归（PCA-GP）等先压缩后预测的流程虽然能处理高维，但其优化基函数是针对重建而非预测，导致预测性能受限。

Method: 将每个输出建模为低维潜在状态（服从高斯过程先验）的线性高斯解码，通过解析边际化解码器权重，将压缩与预测耦合在一个可扩展至高位输出的统一目标函数中。

Result: 成功构建了首个空间分辨的岩石质系外行星全球气候模型仿真器。

Conclusion: GPLFR通过联合优化压缩与预测，克服了传统方法的局限，在高维输出回归任务中表现优异，尤其适用于数据稀缺的科学场景。

Abstract: In the sciences, regression tasks often require predicting high-dimensional outputs from few training examples. Multi-output Gaussian processes excel in low-data regimes but typically struggle with high-dimensional outputs. Compress-then-predict pipelines such as PCA-GP (principal component analysis plus Gaussian process regression) handle high dimensionality, but rely on bases optimized for reconstruction rather than prediction. To address this gap, we propose a model that represents each output as a linear-Gaussian decoding of a low-dimensional latent state drawn from a Gaussian process prior. By analytically marginalizing the decoder weights, we couple compression and prediction in a single objective that scales to high-dimensional outputs. We refer to this model as Gaussian process latent factor regression (GPLFR). We demonstrate GPLFR by building the first spatially resolved emulator of global climate models for rocky exoplanets.

</details>


### [174] [Principles and Practice of Deep Representation Learning: or a Mathematical Theory of Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06624)
*San Buchanan,Druv Pai,Peng Wang,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书旨在通过表征学习的视角，揭示大型深度网络（尤其是生成模型）的内部机制，将其从难以理解的"黑箱"转变为可解释、可靠且可控的设计，并通过优化和信息论原理将架构设计从"炼金术"简化为本科水平的线性代数与微积分练习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型（尤其是大规模生成模型）是难以理解的"黑箱"，内部机制不透明，导致可解释性、可靠性和可控性方面的困难，并引发了炒作与恐惧。

Method: 通过表征学习的视角，利用优化理论和信息论来解释现代神经网络架构的设计原则，将架构开发从经验性的"炼金术"简化为基于线性代数和微积分的系统化方法。

Result: 提出新的方法和模型，这些模型在设计上就是高效、可解释和可控的，其性能不逊于甚至超越传统的黑箱模型。

Conclusion: 表征学习是深度学习模型经验性能力中最关键的因素，通过系统化理解其机制，可以设计出既强大又可解释的模型，并为深度学习的未来发展方向提供指导。

Abstract: In the current era of deep learning and especially generative models, there is significant investment in training very large generative models. Thus far, such models have been "black boxes" that are difficult to understand in the sense that they have opaque internal mechanisms, leading to difficulties in interpretability, reliability, and control. Naturally, this lack of understanding has led to both hype and fear.
  This book is an attempt to "open the black box" and understand the mechanisms of large deep networks, through the perspective of representation learning, which is a major factor - arguably the single most important one - in the empirical power of deep learning models. A brief outline of this book is as follows. Chapter 1 will summarize the threads that underlie the whole text. Chapters 2, 3, 4, 5, and 6 will explain the design principles of modern neural network architectures through optimization and information theory, reducing the process of architecture development (long having been described as a sort of "alchemy") to undergraduate-level linear algebra and calculus exercises once the underlying principles are introduced. Chapters 7 and 8 will discuss applications of these principles to solve problems in more paradigmatic ways, obtaining new methods and models which are efficient, interpretable, and controllable by design, and yet no less - sometimes even more - powerful than the black-box models they resemble. Chapter 9 will discuss potential future directions for deep learning, the role of representation learning, as well as some open problems.

</details>


### [175] [The Identity Trap in EEG Foundation Models: A Diagnostic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06647)
*Jun-You Lin,Ying Choon Wu,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG基础模型在subject-disjoint交叉验证中的高准确率可能源于“身份陷阱”（模型学习受试者身份特征而非临床标签）。提出FMScope诊断协议，通过5种诊断工具验证该陷阱普遍存在（冻结特征中受试者方差为随机基线13-89倍），并发现1/f非周期性成分是身份特征的重要载体。消除身份特征后，在标签随受试者变化的场景中标签解码提升6-12个百分点。微调仅在已有跨受试者标记的细胞中放大标签相关方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示EEG基础模型在subject-disjoint交叉验证中高准确率的伪像本质——模型可能依赖受试者身份特征（身份陷阱）而非真实临床生物标记，并开发在微调前即可诊断该问题的工具。

Method: 提出FMScope协议，包含5项诊断：方差分解、受试者轴擦除、非周期性1/f消融、逐层标签探测、受试者内方向一致性。在3个预训练FM（LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE）和4个数据集上，按标签与受试者关系×是否存在共识跨受试者EEG标记的2×2布局进行验证。

Result: (i)身份陷阱普遍存在：12/12数据对中冻结受试者方差为随机基线13-89倍，微调后进一步扩大（+10至+63 pp）；消除身份线性轴后，在标签随受试者变化的场景中标签解码提升6-12 pp（主细胞）及4-27 pp（外部队列）。(ii)非周期性1/f是身份特征载体之一：消融后LaBraM和CBraMod的受试者探测下降9-19 pp，REVE无此依赖。(iii)微调仅在已有文献确证跨受试者标记的细胞中放大标签方差。

Conclusion: 身份陷阱是基于生理基础的捷径学习实例，subject-disjoint分割无法排除该问题。FMScope可区分反映真实生物标记的性能提升与反映受试者身份的性能提升。

Abstract: Objective. EEG foundation models (FMs) report strong accuracy on clinical resting-state EEG. However, high accuracy under subject-disjoint cross-validation remains ambiguous: it can reflect a genuine clinical biomarker, or subject-identity features that correlate with the label. We name this the Identity Trap and ask whether it can be diagnosed at the representation level before fine-tuning.
  Approach. We propose FMScope, a frozen-representation protocol packaging five diagnostics: variance decomposition, subject-axis erasure, aperiodic 1/f ablation, layer-wise label probing, and within-subject direction consistency. We apply it to three pretrained FMs (LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE) across four datasets in a 2x2 layout: subject relation of label x presence of a consensus cross-subject EEG marker.
  Main results. (i) The Identity Trap is universal: frozen subject-variance is 13-89x a random null in 12/12 pairs, rising in all 12 under fine-tuning (+10 to +63 pp). This dominance is a removable linear axis: erasing it improves label decoding where the label varies within subject (+6 to +12 pp in primary cells; +4 to +27 pp across external cohorts). (ii) Aperiodic 1/f is one subject carrier: removing it drops the subject probe by 9-19 pp on LaBraM and CBraMod. REVE saturates subject identity without measurable aperiodic dependence. (iii) Fine-tuning amplifies label-variance only in cells with a literature-established cross-subject marker.
  Significance. The Identity Trap is a physically-grounded instance of shortcut learning: the preferred cue has a measurable physiological component, and subject-disjoint splitting alone cannot rule it out. FMScope separates gains reflecting a biological marker from those reflecting subject identity.

</details>


### [176] [Capturing non-Markovian dynamics in non-equilibrium stochastic systems using flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06658)
*Bhargav Sriram Siddani,John B. Bell,Alejandro L. Garcia,Ishan Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对粗粒化SPDE（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）无法准确捕捉短时间非马尔可夫效应和低密度非高斯分布的问题，本文提出一种生成流匹配方法，直接从粒子模拟中建模通量概率分布，并在Kramers首次通过时间问题中证明该方法比马尔可夫基线更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒化随机偏微分方程（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）在短时间动力学和低粒子密度区域的非高斯分布上存在精度不足的问题。

Method: 提出一种生成流匹配方法，直接从粒子模拟数据中建模通量概率分布，显式纳入非马尔可夫和非高斯效应。

Result: 在非相互作用布朗粒子系统的Kramers首次通过时间问题中，该方法准确捕获了短时间行为，并在数密度统计矩上优于正则化DK方程的解。

Conclusion: 所提出的生成流匹配方法能够有效弥补传统粗粒化SPDE在短时间和低密度场景下的建模缺陷，提供更精确的预测。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic models of stochastic particle systems represented by coarse-grained stochastic partial differential equations (SPDE), such as the regularized Dean-Kawasaki (DK) equation, do not accurately capture the short-time system dynamics that is dominated by non-Markovian effects, and low particle density regimes where the distributions are highly non-Gaussian. We develop a generative flow matching method that directly models the probability distribution of fluxes from particle simulations that explicitly incorporates non-Markovian and non-Gaussian effects. As a demonstration, we use this method to simulate the Kramers first passage time problem for a system of non-interacting Brownian particles. We show the model accurately captures the short-time behavior and provides better predictions of the statistical moments of the number density when compared against the solution of the Markovian baseline, regularized DK equation.

</details>


### [177] [Explainable Runtime Dependency Tracking for AI-RAN Conflict Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06663)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种轻量级监控原语，通过布尔矩阵表示参数-KPI依赖关系，并利用滑动窗口推理从流式遥测事件中跟踪依赖关系的变化，为AI-RAN中的冲突监控提供可解释信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI-RAN中，冲突监控不仅需要知道部署了哪些应用，还需要确认为运行时诊断所假设的参数-KPI依赖关系在当前运行状态下是否仍然有效。

Method: 使用布尔矩阵表示活跃依赖关系，通过布尔矩阵乘法检查参数活动事件与KPI响应事件的一致性；提出滑动窗口推理过程，在一致性保持时复用当前估计，在观测到结构变化时重新计算。

Result: 在受控布尔事件流上的实验表明，该方法能够在依赖关系变化和布尔观测噪声下实现高效且准确的跟踪。

Conclusion: 该跟踪器作为冲突诊断和慢循环模型刷新的可解释信号，而非自主缓解机制，为AI-RAN的可信运行提供了基础监控能力。

Abstract: Future AI-integrated Radio Access Networks (AI-RAN) will combine open programmability with learning-enabled xApps, rApps, and control functions that act on shared parameters and key performance indicators (KPIs). For conflict monitoring, it is not enough to know which applications are deployed; the system must also know whether the parameter--KPI dependencies assumed by runtime diagnosis remain valid under the current operating regime. This paper studies a lightweight monitoring primitive for that purpose: tracking an interpretable dependency representation from streaming telemetry events.
  We represent active dependencies by a Boolean matrix and use Boolean matrix multiplication to check whether recent parameter-activity and KPI-response events are consistent with the current estimate. We propose a sliding-window inference procedure that reuses the estimate when it remains consistent and recomputes it when recent observations indicate structural change. The tracker is intended as an explainable signal for conflict diagnosis and slow-loop model refresh, not as an autonomous mitigation mechanism. Experiments on controlled Boolean event streams show efficient and accurate tracking under dependency changes and Boolean observation noise.

</details>


### [178] [Uncertainty-Aware LLM-Guided Policy Shaping for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06673)
*Ujjwal Bhatta,Utsabi Dangol,Sumaly Bajracharya,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ULPS框架，将校准后的LLM与RL结合，通过A*生成符号轨迹微调BERT，利用MC dropout不确定性估计和熵基混合机制平衡LLM引导与PPO策略，在MiniGridUnlockPickup基准上提升成功率、奖励效率和样本复杂度，执行准确率提升超9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对强化学习中稀疏奖励和异构任务序列导致的收敛慢、泛化弱、探索低效问题，寻求将LLM的结构化先验知识与不确定性感知控制相结合的有效方法。

Method: (1) 基于A*的oracle合成最优符号轨迹；(2) 用符号轨迹微调BERT语言模型；(3) 训练时通过MC dropout估计认知不确定性；(4) 基于熵的混合机制自适应平衡LLM引导与PPO策略。

Result: 在MiniGridUnlockPickup基准上，ULPS相比无引导、未校准和标准RL基线，在成功率、奖励效率和样本复杂度上均有一致提升，执行准确率提升超9%，奖励AUC更高。

Conclusion: 集成符号A*轨迹、预训练语言先验和不确定性感知控制为稀疏奖励多任务RL提供了一种有效方法，且具有扩展至部分可观测和多智能体环境的潜力。

Abstract: Sparse rewards and heterogeneous task sequences remain persistent challenges in Reinforcement Learning (RL), often resulting in slow convergence, weak generalization, and inefficient exploration. We propose Uncertainty-Aware LLM-Guided Policy Shaping (ULPS), a novel framework that integrates a calibrated Large Language Model (LLM) into the RL training loop to provide structured, uncertainty-modulated behavioral guidance. ULPS employs an A*-based oracle to synthesize optimal symbolic trajectories, which are used to fine-tune a BERT-based language model. During training, this model supplies action suggestions whose influence is conditioned on epistemic uncertainty estimated via Monte Carlo (MC) dropout. An entropy-based blending mechanism adaptively balances LLM guidance and the learned policy (via Proximal Policy Optimization, PPO), allowing the agent to prioritize reliable priors while preserving adaptability. We evaluate ULPS on the MiniGridUnlockPickup benchmark and observe consistent improvements in success rate, reward efficiency, and sample complexity over unguided, uncalibrated, and standard RL baselines. ULPS achieves more than 9% improvement in execution accuracy after fine-tuning, requires fewer environment interactions, and yields higher reward AUC. Our results demonstrate that integrating symbolic A* trajectories, pretrained language priors, and uncertainty-aware control offers a principled and effective approach to multi-task reinforcement learning in sparse-reward domains, with potential extensibility to partially observable and multi-agent settings.

</details>


### [179] [Spatiotemporal Imputation with Graph-Informed Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06682)
*Zepeng Zhang,Aref Einizade,Jhony H. Giraldo,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GiFlow提出了一种基于图信息流匹配的时空数据插补框架，用图信息先验替代高斯先验，结合混合向量场模型（空间注意力、时间注意力、时空传播），在合成和真实数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空数据插补方法（如RNN、GNN）依赖迭代传播，易累积误差；扩散方法虽缓解误差传播但需迭代采样且依赖与问题无关的高斯先验，效率和效果受限。

Method: 提出Graph-Informed Flow Matching（GiFlow）框架：(1) 用时空滤波从可观测信号构建图信息先验替代高斯先验，使源分布更接近目标分布，简化生成轨迹；(2) 流场由混合向量场模型参数化，集成空间注意力、时间注意力和时空传播，联合建模时空依赖。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，GiFlow在时空插补任务上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GiFlow通过图信息先验和混合向量场模型有效解决了现有方法的误差累积和先验不匹配问题，提升了时空插补的性能与效率。

Abstract: Missing data is a common challenge in spatiotemporal systems, arising in applications such as air quality monitoring and urban traffic management. Traditional machine learning approaches, like recurrent and graph neural networks, rely on iterative propagation, which tends to accumulate errors over time and space. Recent diffusion-based methods mitigate error propagation but require iterative sampling and often depend on problem-agnostic Gaussian priors, limiting both efficiency and effectiveness. To address these limitations, we propose GiFlow, a Graph-Informed Flow Matching framework for spatiotemporal imputation. GiFlow replaces the typical Gaussian prior with a graph-informed prior constructed via spatiotemporal filtering of observable signals, which better aligns the source distribution to the target and thereby simplifies the generation trajectory. The flow field is parameterized by a hybrid vector field model that integrates spatial attention, temporal attention, and spatiotemporal propagation, enabling joint modeling of spatial and temporal dependencies. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed GiFlow outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in spatiotemporal imputation. The code is available at https://github.com/zepengzhang/GiFlow.

</details>


### [180] [Towards Serverless Semi-Decentralized Federated Learning with Heterogeneous Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06687)
*Su Wang,Mung Chiang,H. Vincent Poor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SSD-FL，一种无需持久服务器的半去中心化联邦学习框架，通过轻量级D2D初始化阶段实现聚类形成，并利用有效损失函数和Cheeger不等式驱动的迭代聚类算法，在异构ML优化器环境下提升收敛速度和通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化FL中网络图结构、本地数据异质性和不同ML优化器之间的复杂耦合使得聚类形成优化极具挑战，而现有研究尚未充分探索完全去中心化环境下的聚类价值与发展。

Method: 提出SSD-FL，包含：1) 轻量级一次性D2D初始化阶段完成聚类；2) 将全局轮次分割为簇内和簇间阶段，通过整合设备特定ML优化器与网络图正则化的"有效损失函数"确保全局收敛与共识；3) 利用Cheeger不等式通过共识间隙开发迭代聚类算法，并引入量化数据与优化器异质性的评分指标。

Result: 实验验证SSD-FL在多种网络图、数据集和本地优化器设置下，相比三类去中心化FL方法，在收敛速度和通信效率上均有提升。

Conclusion: SSD-FL有效解决了去中心化FL中异构优化器环境下的聚类形成难题，为无服务器基础设施的联邦学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We investigate cluster formation, involving the number and composition of clusters, in decentralized federated learning (FL) with heterogeneous machine learning (ML) optimizers. While clustering in centralized FL has enabled scalability and resource savings, its value and development in fully decentralized environments have yet to be explored. Optimizing cluster formation in such environments is challenging, especially due to the complex coupling between network graph structures, local data heterogeneity, and different local ML model optimizers. To address these challenges, we propose serverless semi-decentralized FL (SSD-FL), a methodology requiring no persistent server infrastructure. In SSD-FL, cluster formation occurs via a lightweight, one-time device-to-device (D2D) initialization phase, after which actual ML model training (alongside consensus and convergence processes) is fully serverless. Functionally, SSD-FL segments global rounds into intra-cluster and inter-cluster regimes, ensuring global convergence and consensus through novel "effective loss functions" that integrate device-specific ML optimizers with network graph-based regularization. Next, SSD-FL leverages the consensus gap via the Cheeger inequality to develop an iterative clustering algorithm evaluated against our derived convergence and consensus bounds, which incorporate a unique scoring metric to quantify data and optimizer heterogeneity across devices. Finally, experimental evaluation against three categories of decentralized FL methodologies validate that SSD-FL improves both convergence speeds and communication efficiency across various network graphs, datasets, and local optimizer regimes.

</details>


### [181] [The Geography of Algorithmic Judgment: LLM Intermediaries, Place Identity, and Racial Steering in Housing Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06694)
*Hana Samad,Trung Lam,Christoph Mügge-Durum,Michael Akinwumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对七种大语言模型在美国四个城市的行为审计，发现LLM在住房推荐中表现出种族导向（steering）行为，且该行为是模型解释自由度的涌现特性，而非静态属性。导向行为取决于用户身份、偏好表达与模型内化空间逻辑的交互作用，且在不同城市间不具有泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益成为住房搜索的中介角色，通过对话界面整合房源平台，影响城市环境中的信息获取、搜索和推荐。先前研究已发现LLM中存在种族导向问题，本研究旨在系统性地审计并理解该行为的本质与机制。

Method: 对七种开放权重和闭源LLM在美国四个城市进行行为审计，采用三种迭代提示条件（逐步增加生活方式偏好语境），并借鉴公平住房配对测试方法论，测试位置推荐。

Result: 导向是模型解释自由度的涌现行为，而非静态属性；其源于用户身份、偏好表达与模型内化的空间逻辑之间的交互；导向方向与幅度在不同条件下不一致；偏好条件测试相对于基线条件增加或重构了表现出导向行为的模型数量；城市不是中立的测试单元，结果不能跨市场泛化。

Conclusion: 住房领域采用AI工具时，需要本地和领域专业知识以确保公平住房的法律和制度承诺不受损害。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly assuming an intermediary role in housing search through the integration of listing platforms within conversational interfaces, mediating access to information, search, and recommendations within urban settings. We expand on prior work on racial steering in LLMs by conducting a behavioral audit of seven open-weight and closed-source LLMs across four U.S. cities, testing location recommendations across three iterative prompting conditions that progressively add lifestyle preference context and reflect fair housing paired-testing methodologies. We find that steering is an emergent behavior of the model's interpretive license rather than primarily a static property. Steering results from the interaction of a user's identity, preference articulation, and the spatial logic that a model has internalized about learned representations of place, preference, and opportunity in a given city, and how different types of users relate to it. While steering was present, it was not uniform in direction or magnitude across evaluated conditions. Preference-conditioned testing often increased or reconfigured the number of models that exhibited steering behaviors relative to baseline conditions, suggesting that LLMs may interpret what the same housing preference means differently depending on the racial identity of the user. Our findings also demonstrate that the city is not a neutral testing unit for LLM evaluation in place-based sectors, and results from one local market cannot be assumed to generalize to another. Local and domain expertise will be required in the housing sector to ensure that legal and institutional commitments to fair housing are not undermined while adopting AI tools that mediate spatial access.

</details>


### [182] [RECAP: Regression Evaluation for Continual Adaptation of Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06698)
*Harsh Deshpande,Kushal Chawla,Sangwoo Cho,William Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAP是一个衡量在严格主动适应-测试协议下（仅提供约束规范，未见测试数据前必须泛化）约束级持续学习现象（遗忘、回归、前向迁移）的基准。评估6种方法在4个LLM和3种调度下的表现，发现这些方法在性能上无显著提升，即使付出更高延迟成本。现有离线或反应式方法不适应主动范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的智能体系统经常面临不断变化的约束（如工具调用通知改变合规阈值、政策更新增加披露要求），且必须在下一个交互中立即遵守，几乎没有容错空间。这种主动适应场景在部署中很常见，但现有基准只考虑静态约束集或带有评估反馈的反应式协议，缺乏对此类场景的评估。

Method: 提出RECAP基准，采用严格的主动适应-测试协议：提示优化方法仅接收约束规范，必须在看到任何测试数据之前进行泛化。在约束级别衡量持续学习现象（遗忘、回归、前向迁移）。跨6种方法、4个LLM和3种不断演变约束的调度进行系统评估。

Result: 现有方法在性能上无显著提升，即使增加延迟成本也无济于事。这些设计用于离线或反应式设置的方法无法适应主动范式。

Conclusion: 强调亟需设计主动提示适应方法，使模型在部署中面对不断变化的需求时保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Production agentic systems routinely face evolving constraints and must comply from the very next interaction. Scenarios like a tool-call notification changing a compliance threshold or a policy update adding disclosure requirements fit this criteria, having close to no room for errors in production. This proactive adaptation setting is common in deployment, but absent from current benchmarks, which assume either static constraint sets or reactive protocols with evaluation feedback. We introduce RECAP, a benchmark that measures continual-learning phenomena (forgetting, regression, forward transfer) at the constraint level under a strictly proactive adapt-then-test protocol: prompt optimization methods receive only the constraint specification and must generalize before seeing any test data. Evaluating six methods across four LLMs and three schedules with evolving constraints, we find that these methods show no significant improvement in performance, even after incurring a higher latency. These methods, designed for offline or reactive settings, are inadequate for the proactive paradigm. Our work emphasizes the growing need for designing proactive prompt adaptation methods, where the models must remain robust to evolving needs in deployment.

</details>


### [183] [ShallowBench: Benchmarking Generative Drug Design Models on Shallow-Pocket Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06717)
*Saket Reddy,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ShallowBench基准，包含5,780个浅口袋靶点，用于评估生成式AI模型在低可成靶性蛋白质（如KRAS和MYC）上的配体采样能力。通过Alpha Shape"盖子"体积与蛋白质原子体素体积的差值来分离低凹度靶点，实验表明现有模型在这些靶点上预测结合亲和力较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI模型在基于结构的药物设计中主要依赖深结合口袋，难以对浅口袋（低可成靶性）靶点（如KRAS、MYC等历史上"不可成药"的肿瘤靶点）进行有效的配体采样，因此需要专门针对这类挑战性靶点的基准来推动方法创新。

Method: 从CrossDocked2020中严格筛选出5,780个浅口袋靶点；通过计算Alpha Shape"盖子"体积与底层蛋白质原子体素体积的差值来分离低凹度（low concavity）但表面积足够的靶点。

Result: 评估多种当前最先进的生成模型，发现它们在这些低凹度界面上预测的结合亲和力较弱。

Conclusion: ShallowBench为生成式生物学模型提供了一个严格的基准，突出了需要新的架构设计或损失函数来应对这些具有挑战性的靶点。

Abstract: While generative AI models have demonstrated remarkable success in structure-based drug design, they predominantly rely on deep binding pockets and struggle to sample effective ligands for challenging low-pocketability targets, such as the historically "undruggable" oncology targets KRAS and MYC. To address this gap, we introduce ShallowBench, a strictly curated benchmark of 5,780 shallow-pocket targets extracted from CrossDocked2020. By computing the difference between an Alpha Shape "lid" volume and the underlying protein atom voxel volume, we successfully isolated targets with low concavity while ensuring sufficient surface area for binding. Evaluating various state-of-the-art generative models reveals weaker predicted binding affinity on these low-concavity interfaces.
